{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"09338746-ab51-40b7-a30b-3e797eb69648","agentId":"afb517f0e340c4912","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"你是资深代码审计专家。对 Orion 平台的 **工作台 (/workbench) + 控制台 (/console)** 两个菜单模块进行深度能力评估。\n\n【铁律】\n1. 质量 > 数量。宁可深度分析 3 个页面，不要浅扫 50 个模块\n2. 每条结论必须附带 file:line 证据\n3. 禁止仅通过文件名推断功能，必须读取代码确认\n4. 看到 setTimeout/硬编码数组/注释掉的不算实现\n5. 每条 API 调用必须追踪到后端路由+Controller+Service+Repository\n\n【必须完成的 6 个任务】\n1. 路由注册检查：routes.ts 是否 import 并 register？未注册 = 404\n2. 完整调用链追踪：按钮 → handle → API → 路由 → Controller → Service → Repository → DB，每步标注 ✅/❌/⚠️\n3. 代码级验证：setTimeout=Mock、硬编码=Mock、catch降级、Map=非持久化\n4. 4 维度评分：代码完整度/持久化/API对接/交互完整\n5. 场景逆向验证：选一个真实流程走完，记录断在哪个环节\n6. 输出缺失能力清单（P0/P1/P2 分级）\n\n【输出格式】\n```markdown\n# [模块名] 深度能力评估报告\n\n## 一、模块概览\n| 指标 | 值 |\n|------|-----|\n| 页面数 | X |\n| 代码行数 | X |\n| 后端 Service 数 | X |\n| API 路由注册状态 | ✅/❌/⚠️ |\n\n## 二、交互完整度\n| 页面 | 异步函数数 | 缺 loading | 缺 feedback | 缺 empty | 缺 confirm |\n\n## 三、样式规范符合度\n| 页面 | 颜色违规 | 圆角违规 | 间距违规 | 阴影违规 | 标题不规范 |\n\n## 四、逻辑完整度（含调用链追踪）\n| 数据实体 | Create | Read | Update | Delete | 调用链状态 |\n\n## 五、功能缺失识别\n| 页面 | 缺编辑 | 缺删除 | 缺搜索 | 缺导出 | 缺报表 | 缺权限 |\n\n## 六、微前端拆分评估\n| 评估项 | 评分(1-5) | 依据 |\n|--------|----------|------|\n| 代码规模 | | |\n| 团队独立性 | | |\n| 技术栈差异 | | |\n| 部署频率 | | |\n| 故障隔离 | | |\n| 独立后端 | | |\n| **综合建议** | | |\n\n## 七、场景逆向验证\n场景：\"[描述]\"\n1. 用户点击 → ✅/❌\n2. 前端 API → ✅/❌\n3. 后端路由 → ✅/❌\n4. Controller → ✅/❌\n5. Service → ✅/❌\n6. 持久化 → ✅/❌\n7. 前端反馈 → ✅/❌\n卡在第 X 步：[说明]\n\n## 八、缺失能力清单\n### P0\n### P1\n### P2\n```\n\n【规范来源】\n- `docs/规范汇总/Orion统一规范汇总.md`\n- `orion-frontend/src/tokens/colors.ts` / `radius.ts` / `spacing.ts`\n\n【工作目录】/Users/heal/orion-design\n\n开始执行。先列出要扫描的文件清单，然后逐页深度分析。"},"uuid":"7982dddc-12fc-4cd7-8039-4e7b2672fbbb","timestamp":"2026-05-22T07:54:41.410Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}

{"parentUuid":"38e63970-9b8b-46d0-bd1f-45a17cfa30d8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"09338746-ab51-40b7-a30b-3e797eb69648","agentId":"afb517f0e340c4912","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_0edfa9318bd44cec85b4bedf","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Workbench API Routes — Personal unified dashboard backend\n3\t *\n4\t * GET /api/v1/workbench — Aggregate pipeline/alerts/tickets/deployments\n5\t */\n6\t\n7\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n8\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n9\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n10\timport { WorkbenchService } from '../services/workbench/WorkbenchService';\n11\timport { DatabasePool } from '../services/database';\n12\t\n13\tinterface WorkbenchRoutesOptions {\n14\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n15\t}\n16\t\n17\texport default async function workbenchRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, options: WorkbenchRoutesOptions): Promise<void> {\n18\t  const pool = options.database;\n19\t  const workbenchService = pool ? new WorkbenchService(pool) : undefined;\n20\t\n21\t  // GET /api/v1/workbench — Personal aggregated workbench\n22\t  app.get('/', {\n23\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'workbench', action: 'read' })],\n24\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n25\t    if (!workbenchService) {\n26\t      return reply.status(503).send({ error: 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE', message: 'Database not connected' });\n27\t    }\n28\t\n29\t    const query = request.query as { tenantId?: string; userId?: string };\n30\t    const tenantId = query.tenantId || 'default';\n31\t    const userId = query.userId || (request as any).user?.id || 'anonymous';\n32\t\n33\t    const data = await workbenchService.getWorkbench(userId, tenantId);\n34\t    return reply.send({ success: true, data });\n35\t  });\n36\t}\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"d0e47d8c-0b48-421e-8078-59db9f483a13","timestamp":"2026-05-22T07:56:46.352Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"38e63970-9b8b-46d0-bd1f-45a17cfa30d8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}

},\n165\t    disasterRecovery: { enabled: true, autoStart: true },\n166\t    selfHealing: { enabled: true, autoStart: true },\n167\t    ticketing: { enabled: true, autoStart: true },\n168\t features: {},\n190\t};\n191\t\n192\texport interface ApiRoutesOptions {\n193\t  eventBus?: EventBusService;\n194\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n195\t  redis?: RedisCache;\n196\t  /** Enable four-layer tenant isolation */\n197\t  enableTenantIsolation?: boolean;\n198\t}\n199\t\n200\t// 角色常量 — 集中管理受保护路由所需的角色\n201\tconst ADMIN_ROLES = ['admin', 'platform_admin'] as const;\n202\t\n203\t/**\n204\t * 初始化租户隔离服务\n205\t */\n206\tfunction initializeTenantIsolation(database: DatabasePool | undefined): {\n207\t  isolationService: TenantIsolationService;\n208\t  rlsPolicyManager: RLSPolicyManager | null;\n209\t} {\n210\t  const isolationService = new TenantIsolationService();\n211\t\n212\t  let rlsPolicyManager: RLSPolicyManager | null = null;\n213\t  if (database) {\n214\t    rlsPolicyManager = new RLSPolicyManager(database);\n215\t  }\n216\t\n217\t  return { isolationService, rlsPolicyManager };\n218\t}\n219\t\n220\t/**\n221\t * 为路由模块注册带 JWT 认证 + 角色校验的封装插件。\n222\t *\n223\t * Fastify 的插件封装机制确保 addHook 注册的 onRequest 钩子\n224\t * 仅作用于该插件内部注册的路由，不影响其他路由。\n225\t *\n226\t * @param app - Fastify 实例\n227\t * @param routeModule - 路由模块函数\n228\t * @param prefix - 路由前缀（如 '/users'）\n229\t * @param routeOptions - 传递给路由模块的自定义选项（不含 prefix）\n230\t * @param requiredRoles - 所需角色列表\n231\t */\n232\tasync function registerWithRoleGuard(\n233\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n234\t  routeModule: (instance: FastifyInstance, opts?: any) => Promise<void>,\n235\t  prefix: string,\n236\t  routeOptions?: Record<string, unknown>,\n237\t  _requiredRoles: readonly string[] = ADMIN_ROLES\n238\t): Promise<void> {\n239\t  // 已迁移到 registerWithPermission：各路由内部使用 requirePermission 进行细粒度权限控制\n240\t  return registerWithPermission(app, routeModule, prefix, routeOptions);\n241\t}\n242\t\n243\t/**\n244\t * 使用 requirePermission 的模块级注册（细粒度权限控制）\n245\t * @param routeModule - 路由模块函数\n246\t * @param prefix - 路由前缀\n247\t * @param routeOptions - 传递给路由模块的选项\n248\t * @param resourceType - 资源类型（如 'user', 'pipeline'）\n249\t * @param defaultAction - 默认操作（如 'read', 'write'）\n250\t */\n251\tasync function registerWithPermission(\n252\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n253\t  routeModule: (instance: FastifyInstance, opts?: any) => Promise<void>,\n254\t  prefix: string,\n255\t  routeOptions?: Record<string, unknown>,\n256\t  resourceType?: string,\n257\t  defaultAction?: string\n258\t): Promise<void> {\n259\t  await app.register(async (instance: FastifyInstance) => {\n260\t    // Skip auth in development for easier testing\n261\t    const isDev = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development';\n262\t    if (!isDev) {\n263\t      instance.addHook('onRequest', authenticateUser);\n264\t    }\n265\t    // 具体的 requirePermission 在各路由内部添加\n266\t    await instance.register(routeModule, { prefix, ...routeOptions });\n267\t  });\n268\t}\n269\t\n270\texport default async function apiRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, options: ApiRoutesOptions): Promise<void> {\n271\t  // ==================== SSE Service Initialization (shared with pipeline engine) ====================\n272\t  const pipelineLogSSE = new PipelineLogSSEService(new EventEmitter());\n273\t\n274\t  // ==================== LLM Trace Service Initialization ====================\n275\t  initLLMTrace(options.database);\n276\t\n277\t  // ==================== 租户隔离服务初始化 ====================\n278\t  // 初始化四层租户隔离服务 (P0 Task 6)\n279\t  const { isolationService, rlsPolicyManager } = initializeTenantIsolation(options.database);\n280\t\n281\t  // 注册全局租户验证中间件\n282\t  if (options.enableTenantIsolation !== false) {\n283\t    // Layer 1: API层 - TenantValidatorMiddleware\n284\t    const tenantValidatorMiddleware = createTenantValidatorMiddleware(isolationService, {\n285\t      required: true,\n286\t      skipPaths: ['/healthz', '/readyz', '/version', '/api/v1/info', '/api/v1/public', '/metrics'],\n287\t    });\n288\t\n289\t    app.addHook('onRequest', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n290\t      return tenantValidatorMiddleware(request, reply, () => {});\n291\t    });\n292\t\n293\t    // Layer 4: Database RLS — 请求级连接 + AsyncLocalStorage\n294\t    // 替代原有的 pool.query() 方式，确保每个请求使用专用连接，\n295\t    // RLS session variable 在同一连接上设置和查询。\n296\t    if (options.database && rlsPolicyManager) {\n297\t      app.addHook('preHandler', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n298\t        const tenantCtx = (request as any).tenantContext;\n299\t        if (!tenantCtx) return;\n300\t        const tenant = tenantCtx.getCurrentTenant();\n301\t        if (!tenant) return;\n302\t\n303\t        // 从连接池获取专用连接\n304\t        const client = await options.database!.getConnection();\n305\t\n306\t        try {\n307\t          // 在该连接上设置 RLS session variable（SET SESSION 级别）\n308\t          await client.query(\n309\t            \"SELECT set_config('app.current_tenant_id', $1, false), set_config('app.tenant_isolation', $2, false)\",\n310\t            [String(tenant.tenantId), 'true']\n311\t          );\n312\t\n313\t          // 将 client 挂载到 request 上，供 ALS enterWith 使用\n314\t          (request as any).dbClient = client;\n315\t\n316\t          // 进入 ALS 上下文，后续所有 async 调用（包括 handler、service、repository）\n317\t          // 的 DatabasePool.query() 都会自动使用此 client\n318\t          tenantContextStorage.enterWith({\n319\t            dbClient: client,\n320\t            tenantId: tenant.tenantId,\n321\t          });\n322\t        } catch (error) {\n323\t          // set_config 失败 → 拒绝请求（安全失败模式）\n324\t          try {\n325\t            client.release();\n326\t          } catch {}\n327\t          reply.code(403).send({ error: 'Tenant isolation setup failed' });\n328\t          throw new Error('Failed to set tenant context');\n329\t        }\n330\t      });\n331\t\n332\t      // 成功响应时清理连接\n333\t      app.addHook('onResponse', async (request: FastifyRequest) => {\n334\t        const client = (request as any).dbClient as PoolClient | undefined;\n335\t        if (client) {\n336\t          try {\n337\t            // 先 ROLLBACK（幂等，无活跃事务时无影响），确保连接不在 aborted 状态\n338\t            await client.query('ROLLBACK').catch(() => {});\n339\t            // 清除 session 变量\n340\t            await client.query(\n341\t              \"SELECT set_config('app.current_tenant_id', $1, false), set_config('app.tenant_isolation', $2, false)\",\n342\t              ['', 'false']\n343\t            );\n344\t          } finally {\n345\t            client.release();\n346\t          }\n347\t        }\n348\t      });\n349\t\n350\t      // 错误时兜底释放连接（防止客户端断开导致连接泄漏）\n351\t      app.addHook('onError', async (request: FastifyRequest) => {\n352\t        const client = (request as any).dbClient as PoolClient | undefined;\n353\t        if (client) {\n354\t          try {\n355\t            await client.query('ROLLBACK').catch(() => {});\n356\t            client.release();\n357\t          } catch {}\n358\t        }\n359\t      });\n360\t\n361\t      // 客户端断开时释放连接\n362\t      app.addHook('onTimeout', async (request: FastifyRequest) => {\n363\t        const client = (request as any).dbClient as PoolClient | undefined;\n364\t        if (client) {\n365\t          try {\n366\t            client.release();\n367\t          } catch {}\n368\t        }\n369\t      });\n370\t    }\n371\t\n372\t    console.log('[Routes] Four-layer tenant isolation enabled');\n373\t  }\n374\t\n375\t  // ==================== ModuleManager 初始化 ====================\n376\t  const moduleManager = new ModuleManager(() => {\n377\t    const configSvc = (options as any).config || (global as any).unifiedConfigService;\n378\t    if (configSvc?.get) {\n379\t      return configSvc.get('moduleConfig') || DEFAULT_MODULE_CONFIG;\n380\t    }\n381\t    return DEFAULT_MODULE_CONFIG;\n382\t  });\n383\t  moduleManager.loadFromConfig();\n384\t  (options as any).moduleManager = moduleManager;\n385\t\n386\t  // CMDB 路由已迁移到独立 Go 服务 (orion-cmdb-service)\n387\t\n388\t  // 注册 Build Environment API 路由 (PostgreSQL backed for BuildCache)\n389\t  // Code Repository 路由已迁移到 orion-code-svc (port 3010)\n390\t\n391\t  // 注册 Branch Policy API 路由 (PostgreSQL backed)\n392\t  await app.register(branchPolicyRoutes, {\n393\t    prefix: '/code-repo/branch-policies',\n394\t    database: options.database,\n395\t  });\n396\t\n397\t  // 注册 Configuration Management API 路由 (PostgreSQL reply);\n915\t      });\n916\t    });\n917\t\n918\t    // ==================== SCM Webhook routes (public - signature validated) ====================\n919\t    await app.register(async (instance: FastifyInstance) => {\n920\t      // POST /api/v1/webhooks/scm - Receive SCM webhook events\n921\t      instance.post('/webhooks/scm', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n922\t        try {\n923\t          const body = request.body as any;\n924\t          const headers = request.headers as Record<string, string | undefined>;\n925\t\n926\t          const githubSignature = headers['x-hub-signature-256'];\n927\t          const gitlabToken = headers['x-gitlab-token'];\n928\t          const githubEvent = headers['x-github-event'];\n929\t          const gitlabEvent = headers['x-gitlab-event'];\n930\t\n931\t          let event;\n932\t\n933\t          if (githubEvent === 'pull_request') {\n934\t            event = await scmWebhookService.handleGitHubPullRequest(body, githubSignature);\n935\t          } else if (githubSignature || githubEvent) {\n936\t            event = await scmWebhookService.handleGitHubPush(body, githubSignature);\n937\t          } else if (gitlabEvent === 'Merge Request Hook' || gitlabEvent === 'merge_request') {\n938\t            event = await scmWebhookService.handleGitLabMergeRequest(body, gitlabToken);\n939\t          } else if (gitlabToken) {\n940\t            event = await scmWebhookService.handleGitLabPush(body, gitlabToken);\n941\t          } else {\n942\t            event = await scmWebhookService.handleGitHubPush(body);\n943\t          }\n944\t\n945\t          await reply.status(200).send({\n946\t            received: true,\n947\t            eventId: event.id,\n948\t            provider: event.provider,\n949\t            eventType: event.eventType,\n950\t            matchedPipelines: event.matchedPipelines,\n951\t          });\n952\t        } catch (error: any) {\n953\t          await reply.status(401).send({\n954\t            error: 'WEBHOOK_VALIDATION_FAILED',\n955\t            message: error.message,\n956\t          });\n957\t        }\n958\t      });\n959\t\n960\t      // GET /api/v1/webhooks/scm/events - Get webhook event history (auth protected)\n961\t      instance.addHook('onRequest', authenticateUser);\n962\t\n963\t      instance.get('/webhooks/scm/events', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n964\t        const query = request.query as any;\n965\t        const limit = query.limit ? parseInt(query.limit, 10) : 20;\n966\t        const events = scmWebhookService.getEvents(limit);\n967\t\n968\t        await reply.send({\n969\t          data: events.map(e => ({\n970\t            id: e.id,\n971\t            provider: e.provider,\n972\t            eventType: e.eventType,\n973\t            repository: e.repository,\n974\t            branch: e.branch,\n975\t            commitSha: e.commitSha,\n976\t            pusher: e.pusher,\n977\t            timestamp: e.timestamp,\n978\t            matchedPipelines: e.matchedPipelines,\n979\t          })),\n980\t          total: events.length,\n981\t        });\n982\t      });\n983\t    });\n984\t  }\n985\t\n986\t  // Pipeline Graph - YAML/JSON conversion and validation\n987\t  if (options.database) {\n988\t    const pipelineRepository = new Paragraph } = Typography;\n46\t\n47\t// ---- Type definitions ----\n48\t\n49\tinterface PipelineRecord {\n50\t  key: string;\n51\t  name: string;\n52\t  pipelineId: string;\n53\t  status: string;\n73\t}\n74\t\n75\tinterface QuickAction {\n76\t  name: string;\n77\t  icon: React.ReactNode;\n78\t  color: string;\n79\t  path: string;\n80\t}\n81\t\n82\tinterface DashboardLink {\n83\t  name: string;\n84\t  icon: React.ReactNode;\n85\t  color: string;\n86\t  path: string;\n87\t  desc: string;\n88\t}\n89\t\n90\tconst dashboardLinks: DashboardLink[] = [\n91\t  {\n92\t    name: '总览看板',\n93\t    icon: <DashboardOutlined />,\n94\t    color: colors.primary[500],\n95\t    path: '/dashboard/executive',\n96\t    desc: '全局 KPI、趋势、排行',\n97\t  },\n98\t  {\n99\t    name: '经理看板',\n100\t    icon: <TeamOutlined />,\n101\t    color: colors.purple[500],\n102\t    path: '/dashboard/manager',\n103\t    desc: '团队明细、周环比',\n104\t  },\n105\t  {\n106\t    name: '个人看板',\n107\t    icon: <UserSwitchOutlined />,\n108\t    color: colors.success[500],\n109\t    path: '/dashboard/engineer',\n110\t    desc: '个人效能、在手工单',\n111\t  },\n112\t  {\n113\t    name: '告警中心',\n114\t    icon: <AlertOutlined />,\n115\t    color: colors.error[400],\n116\t    path: '/alerts',\n117\t    desc: '告警列表、确认处理',\n118\t  },\n119\t];\n120\t\n121\tconst quickActions: QuickAction[] = [\n122\t  {\n123\t    name: '创建 Pipeline',\n124\t    icon: <RocketOutlined />,\n125\t    color: colors.primary[500],\n126\t    path: '/pipelines/new',\n127\t  },\n128\t  { name: '运行记录', icon: <HistoryOutlined />, color: colors.success[500], path: '/pipeline-runs' },\n129\t  { name: '部署管理', icon: <PlayCircleOutlined />, color: colors.purple[500], path: '/deployments' },\n130\t  { name: '告警管理', icon: <AlertOutlined />, color: colors.warning[500], path: '/alerts' },\n131\t];\n132\t\n133\tconst statusColors: Record<string, string> = {\n134\t  running: 'processing',\n135\t  success: 'success',\n136\t  failed: 'error',\n137\t  pending: 'warning',\n138\t  healthy: 'success',\n139\t  warning: 'warning',\n140\t  error: 'error',\n141\t  cancelled: 'default',\n142\t};\n143\t\n144\tconst priorityColors: Record<string, string> = {\n145\t  high: 'red',\n146\t  medium: 'orange',\n147\t  low: 'blue',\n148\t};\n149\t\n150\t// Helper: format duration\n151\tconst formatDuration = (run: PipelineRun): string => {\n152\t  if (run.duration) {\n153\t    const seconds = run.duration / 1000;\n154\t    if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds.toFixed(1)}s`;\n155\t    const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n156\t    const remainingSeconds = Math.floor(seconds % 60);\n157\t    return `${minutes}m ${remainingSeconds}s`;\n158\t  }\n159\t  return '-';\n160\t};\n161\t\n162\t// Helper: format time relative to now\n163\tconst formatTimeRelative = (timeStr?: string): string => {\n164\t  if (!timeStr) return '-';\n165\t  return dayjs(timeStr).locale('zh-cn').fromNow();\n166\t};\n167\t\n168\t// Helper: map trigger type to display\n169\tconst formatTrigger = (trigger: string): string => {\n170\t  const map: Record<string, string> = {\n171\t    manual: '手动',\n172\t    push: '代码推送',\n173\t    schedule: '定时',\n174\t    api: 'API',\n175\t  };\n176\t  return map[trigger] || trigger;\n177\t};\n178\t\n179\tconst DashboardNew: React.FC = () => {\n180\t  const navigate = useNavigate();\n181\t\n182\t  // State\n183\t  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n184\t  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n185\t  const [pipelines, setPipelines] = useState<PipelineRun[]>([]);\n186\t  const [recentRuns, setRecentRuns] = useState<PipelineRun[]>([]);\n187\t  const [systemHealth, setSystemHealth] = useState<SystemHealthItem[]>([]);\n188\t\n189\t  // Derived stats from real data\n190\t  const pipelineStats = {\n191\t    total: pipelines.length,\n192\t    running: recentRuns.filter((r) => r.status === 'running').length,\n193\t    success: recentRuns.filter((r) => r.status === 'success').length,\n194\t    failed: recentRuns.filter((r) => r.status === 'failed').length,\n195\t    pending: recentRuns.filter((r) => r.status === 'pending').length,\n196\t  };\n197\t\n198\t  // Mock tasks (until tasks API is available)\n199\t  const tasks: TaskRecord[] = [\n200\t    {\n201\t      key: '1',\n202\t      title: '检查失败的 Pipeline 运行',\n203\t      priority: 'high',\n204\t      status: 'todo',\n205\t      assignee: '当前用户',\n206\t      due: '今天',\n207\t    },\n208\t    {\n209\t      key: '2',\n210\t      title: '处理待确认的告警',\n211\t      priority: 'high',\n212\t      status: 'todo',\n213\t      assignee: '当前用户',\n214\t      due: '今天',\n215\t    },\n216\t    {\n217\t      key: '3',\n218\t      title: 'Review 待审批的部署',\n219\t      priority: 'medium',\n220\t      status: 'todo',\n221\t      assignee: '当前用户',\n222\t      due: '本周',\n223\t    },\n224\t  ];\n225\t\n226\t  const taskStats = {\n227\t    total: tasks.length,\n228\t    inProgress: tasks.filter((t) => t.status === 'in-progress').length,\n229\t    todo: tasks.filter((t) => t.status === 'todo').length,\n230\t    completed: 0,\n231\t  };\n232\t\n233\t  // Load data from APIs\n234\t  const loadData = async () => {\n235\t    setLoading(true);\n236\t    setError(null);\n237\t    try {\n238\t      // Fetch pipelines\n239\t      const pipelinesRes = await getPipelines();\n240\t      if (pipelinesRes.data?.data) {\n241\t        setPipelines(Array.isArray(pipelinesRes.data.data) ? pipelinesRes.data.data : []);\n242\t      }\n243\t\n244\t      // Fetch recent runs\n245\t      const runsRes = await getPipelineRuns('all', { page: 1, pageSize: 5 });\n246\t      if (runsRes.data?.data) {\n247\t        setRecentRuns(Array.isArray(runsRes.data.data) ? runsRes.data.data : []);\n248\t      }\n249\t\n250\t      // Fetch monitoring health\n251\t      try {\n252\t        const healthRes = await getMonitoringHealth();\n253\t        if (healthRes.data?.status) {\n254\t          setSystemHealth([\n255\t            { name: 'API Gateway', status: 'healthy', latency: '-', uptime: '-' },\n256\t            { name: 'Platform Service', status: healthRes.data.status === 'ok' ? 'healthy' : 'warning', latency: '-', uptime: '-' },\n257\t            { name: 'Database', status: 'healthy', latency: '-', uptime: '-' },\n258\t            { name: 'Redis', status: 'healthy', latency: '-', uptime: '-' },\n259\t          ]);\n260\t        }\n261\t      } catch {\n262\t        // Fallback to basic health info\n263\t        setSystemHealth([\n264\t          { name: 'API Gateway', status: 'healthy', latency: '-', uptime: '-' },\n265\t          { name: 'Platform Service', status: 'healthy', latency: '-', uptime: '-' },\n266\t          { name: 'Database', status: 'healthy', latency: '-', uptime: '-' },\n267\t          { name: 'Redis', status: 'healthy', latency: '-', uptime: '-' },\n268\t        ]);\n269\t      }\n270\t    } catch (err) {\n271\t      // Backend endpoints may not all be available; use demo data\n272\t      setError('加载数据失败，使用演示数据展示');\n273\t    } finally {\n274\t      setLoading(false);\n275\t    }\n276\t  };\n277\t\n278\t  useEffect(() => {\n279\t    loadData();\n280\t  }, []);\n281\t\n282\t  // Transform runs to table records\n283\t  const recentPipelineRecords: PipelineRecord[] = recentRuns.map((run, idx) => ({\n284\t    key: String(idx + 1),\n285\t    name: run.pipelineName || run.pipelineId,\n286\t    pipelineId: run.pipelineId,\n287\t    status: run.status,\n288\t    duration: formatDuration(run),\n289\t    trigger: run.author || formatTrigger(run.trigger),\n290\t    time: formatTimeRelative(run.startTime),\n291\t  }));\n292\t\n293\t  const taskColumns: ColumnsType<TaskRecord> = [\n294\t    {\n295\t      title: '任务',\n296\t      dataIndex: 'title',\n297\t      key: 'title',\n298\t      render: (text: string, record: TaskRecord) => (\n299\t        <Space direction=\"vertical\" size={0} style={{ width: '100%' }}>\n300\t          <Text strong>{text}</Text>\n301\t          <Space size={8} style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>\n302\t            <Tag color={priorityColors[record.priority]}>{record.priority.toUpperCase()}</Tag>\n303\t            <Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: spacing[3] }}>\n304\t              截止：{record.due}\n305\t            </Text>\n306\t          </Space>\n307\t        </Space>\n308\t      ),\n309\t    },\n310\t    {\n311\t      title: '状态',\n312\t      dataIndex: 'status',\n313\t      key: 'status',\n314\t      render: (status: string) => {\n315\t        const config: Record<string, { text: string; color: string }> = {\n316\t          'in-progress': { text: '进行中', color: 'blue' },\n317\t          todo: { text: '待开始', color: 'default' },\n318\t          done: { text: '已完成', color: 'green' },\n319\t        };\n320\t        const { text, color } = config[status] || { text: status, color: 'default' };\n321\t        return (\n322\t          <Badge\n323\t            status={color as 'success' | 'processing' | 'error' | 'default' | 'warning'}\n324\t            text={text}\n325\t          />\n326\t        );\n327\t      },\n328\t    },\n329\t    {\n330\t      title: '负责人',\n331\t      dataIndex: 'assignee',\n332\t      key: 'assignee',\n333\t      render: (assignee: string) => (\n334\t        <Text code style={{ fontSize: spacing[3] }}>\n335\t          {assignee}\n336\t        </Text>\n337\t      ),\n338\t    },\n339\t    {\n340\t      title: '操作',\n341\t      key: 'action',\n342\t      render: () => (\n343\t        <Button type=\"link\" size=\"small\">\n344\t          处理\n345\t        </Button>\n346\t      ),\n347\t    },\n348\t  ];\n349\t\n350\t  const pipelineColumns: ColumnsType<PipelineRecord> = [\n351\t    {\n352\t      title: 'Pipeline',\n353\t {\n366\t      title: '状态',\n367\t      dataIndex: 'status',\n368\t      key: 'status',\n369\t      render: (status: string) => (\n370\t        <Badge\n371\t          status={\n372\t            statusColors[status] as 'success' | 'processing' | 'error' | 'default' | 'warning'\n373\t          }\n374\t          text={status}\n375\t        />\n376\t      ),\n377\t    },\n378\t    {\n379\t      title: '耗时',\n380\t      dataIndex: 'duration',\n381\t      key: 'duration',\n382\t    },\n383\t    {\n384\t      title: '触发人',\n385\t      dataIndex: 'trigger',\n386\t      key: 'trigger',\n387\t    },\n388\t    {\n389\t      title: '时间',\n390\t      dataIndex: 'time',\n391\t      key: 'time',\n392\t    },\n393\t    {\n394\t      title: '操作',\n395\t      key: 'action',\n396\t      render: (_: unknown, record: (typeof recentPipelineRecords)[0]) => (\n397\t        <Space>\n398\t          <Button\n399\t            type=\"link\"\n400\t            size=\"small\"\n401\t            disabled={record.status === 'pending'}\n402\t            onClick={() => navigate(`/pipelines/${record.pipelineId}`)}\n403\t          >\n404\t            查看\n405\t          </Button>\n406\t          {record.status === 'failed' && (\n407\t            <Button type=\"link\" size=\"small\" onClick={() => navigate(`/pipeline-runs`)}>\n408\t              重试\n409\t            </Button>\n410\t          )}\n411\t        </Space>\n412\t      ),\n413\t    },\n414\t  ];\n415\t\n416\t  if (loading) {\n417\t    return (\n418\t      <div style={{ padding: 0, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', minHeight: 400, gap: 12 }}>\n419\t        <Spin size=\"large\" />\n420\t        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\">加载数据中...</Typography.Text>\n421\t      </div>\n422\t    );\n423\t  }\n424\t\n425\t  return (\n426\t    <div style={{ padding: 0 }}>\n427\t      {error && (\n428\t        <Alert\n429\t          message=\"提示\"\n430\t          description={error}\n431\t          type=\"warning\"\n432\t          showIcon\n433\t          closable\n434\t          style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}\n435\t        />\n436\t      )}\n437\t\n438\t      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 24 }}>\n439\t        <div>\n440\t          <Title level={2} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>\n441\t <StatCard title=\"运行中\" value={pipelineStats.running} />\n462\t        </Col>\n463\t        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>\n464\t          <StatCard title=\"成功\" value={pipelineStats.success} />\n465\t * 控制台首页 - 管理员专用\n3\t * 功能：系统概览统计卡片 + 快速导航到管理页面\n4\t *\n5\t * 2025-05-18: 从静态数据迁移到真实API调用\n6\t * - 插件: 使用 plugins API\n7\t * - 系统配置: 使用 feature-flags API\n8\t *\n9\t * 2026-05-19: 精简为纯仪表盘页面，插件管理/系统配置/用户管理\n10\t * 已拆分至独立子页面，通过菜单或快捷入口访问\n11\t */\n12\timport React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';\n13\timport {\n14\t  Card,\n15\t  Tag,\n16\t  Space,\n17\t  Button,\n18\t  Typography,\n19\t  Row,\n20\t  Col,\n21\t  Statistic,\n22\t  Progress,\n23\t  message,\n24\t  Tooltip,\n25\t  Alert,\n26\t  Spin,\n27\t} from 'antd';\n28\timport {\n29\t  ControlOutlined,\n30\t  AppstoreOutlined,\n31\t  SettingOutlined,\n32\t  UserOutlined,\n33\t  SafetyCertificateOutlined,\n34\t  CloudServerOutlined,\n35\t  CheckCircleOutlined,\n36\t  ReloadOutlined,\n37\t} from '@ant-design/icons';\n38\timport { colors, spacing } from '@/tokens';\n39\timport { getInstalledPlugins } from '@/api/plugins';\n40\timport { getFeatureFlags } from '@/api/feature-flags';\n41\t\n42\tconst { Title, Text } = Typography;\n43\t\n44\t// ==================== 类型定义 ====================\n45\t\n46\tinterface ConsoleStats {\n47\t  totalPlugins: number;\n48\t  activePlugins: number;\n49\t  totalFlags: number;\n50\t  enabledFlags: number;\n51\t}\n52\t\n53\t// ==================== 组件 ====================\n54\t\n55\tconst Console: React.FC = () => {\n56\t  // 数据状态\n57\t  const [stats, setStats] = useState<ConsoleStats>({\n58\t    totalPlugins: 0,\n59\t    activePlugins: 0,\n60\t    totalFlags: 0,\n61\t    enabledFlags: 0,\n62\t  });\n63\t\n64\t  // 加载状态\n65\t  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n66\t  const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);\n67\t\n68\t  // 错误状态\n69\t  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n70\t\n71\t  // ==================== 数据加载 ====================\n72\t\n73\t  const loadStats = useCallback(async () => {\n74\t    setLoading(true);\n75\t    setError(null);\n76\t    try {\n77\t      const [pluginsRes, flagsRes] = await Promise.all([\n78\t        getInstalledPlugins(),\n79\t        getFeatureFlags(),\n80\t      ]);\n81\t      const plugins = pluginsRes.data || [];\n82\t      const flags = flagsRes.data || [];\n83\t      setStats({\n84\t        totalPlugins: plugins.length,\n85\t        activePlugins: plugins.filter((p: any) => p.status === 'enabled').length,\n86\t        totalFlags: flags.length,\n87\t        enabledFlags: flags.filter((f: any) => f.enabled).length,\n88\t      });\n89\t    } catch (err) {\n90\t      console.error('加载控制台数据失败:', err);\n91\t      setError('部分数据加载失败，使用演示数据');\n92\t      setStats({\n93\t        totalPlugins: 5,\n94\t        activePlugins: 3,\n95\t        totalFlags: 8,\n96\t        enabledFlags: 6,\n97\t      });\n98\t    } finally {\n99\t      setLoading(false);\n100\t      setInitialLoading(false);\n101\t    }\n102\t  }, []);\n103\t\n104\t  useEffect(() => {\n105\t    loadStats();\n106\t  }, [loadStats]);\n107\t\n108\t  // ==================== 事件处理 ====================\n109\t\n110\t  const handleRefresh = () => {\n111\t    setInitialLoading(true);\n112\t    loadStats();\n113\t    message.info('正在刷新数据...');\n114\t  };\n115\t\n116\t  // ==================== 渲染 ====================\n117\t\n118\t  if (initialLoading) {\n119\t    return (\n120\t      <div\n121\t        style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', alignItems: 'center', minHeight: 400 }}\n122\t      >\n123\t        <Spin size=\"large\" tip=\"加载中...\" />\n124\t      </div>\n125\t    );\n126\t  }\n127\t\n128\t  return (\n129\t    <div style={{ padding: 0 }}>\n130\t      {/* 页面标题 */}\n131\t      <div\n132\t        style={{\n133\t          marginBottom: 24,\n134\t          display: 'flex',\n135\t          justifyContent: 'space-between',\n136\t          alignItems: 'flex-start',\n137\t        }}\n138\t      >\n139\t        <div>\n140\t          <Title level={2} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>\n141\t            <ControlOutlined style={{ marginRight: spacing[3], color: colors.purple[500] }} />\n142\t            系统控制台\n143\t          </Title>\n144\t          <Text type=\"secondary\">管理系统插件、配置和功能开关</Text>\n145\t        </div>\n146\t        <Button\n147\t          icon={<ReloadOutlined />}\n148\t          onClick={handleRefresh}\n149\t          loading={loading}\n150\t        >\n151\t          刷新\n152\t        </Button>\n153\t      </div>\n154\t\n155\t      {/* 错误提示 */}\n156\t      {error && (\n157\t        <Alert\n158\t          message=\"数据加载提示\"\n159\t          description={error}\n160\t          type=\"warning\"\n161\t          showIcon\n162\t          style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}\n163\t        />\n164\t      )}\n165\t\n166\t      {/* 统计卡片区 */}\n167\t      <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>\n168\t        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>\n169\t          <Card hoverable onClick={() => window.location.href = '/console/plugins'}>\n170\t            <Statistic\n171\t              title=\"已安装插件\"\n172\t              value={stats.totalPlugins}\n173\t              prefix={<AppstoreOutlined />}\n174\t              valueStyle={{ color: colors.primary[500] }}\n175\t            />\n176\t            <Progress\n177\t              percent={stats.totalPlugins > 0 ? (stats.activePlugins / stats.totalPlugins) * 100 : 0}\n178\t              strokeColor={colors.primary[500]}\n179\t              size=\"small\"\n180\t              style={{ marginTop: 12 }}\n181\t              format={() => `${stats.activePlugins} 个运行中`}\n182\t            />\n183\t          </Card>\n184\t        </Col>\n185\t        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>\n186\t          <Card hoverable onClick={() => window.location.href = '/console/settings'}>\n187\t            <Statistic\n188\t              title=\"功能开关\"\n189\t              value={stats.totalFlags}\n190\t              prefix={<SettingOutlined />}\n191\t              valueStyle={{ color: colors.purple[500] }}\n192\t            />\n193\t            <Progress\n194\t              percent={stats.totalFlags > 0 ? (stats.enabledFlags / stats.totalFlags) * 100 : 0}\n195\t              strokeColor={colors.purple[500]}\n196\t              size=\"small\"\n197\t              style={{ marginTop: 12 }}\n198\t              format={() => `${stats.enabledFlags} 个已启用`}\n199\t            />\n200\t          </Card>\n201\t        </Col>\n202\t        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>\n203\t          <Card hoverable onClick={() => window.location.href = '/console/users'}>\n204\t            <Statistic\n205\t              title=\"系统用户\"\n206\t              value=\"-\"\n207\t              prefix={<UserOutlined />}\n208\t              valueStyle={{ color: colors.primary[500] }}\n209\t            />\n210\t            <div style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>\n211\t              <Tag color=\"blue\">管理用户 →</Tag>\n212\t            </div>\n213\t          </Card>\n214\t        </Col>\n215\t        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>\n216\t          <Card>\n217\t            <Statistic\n218\t              title=\"系统健康度\"\n219\t              value={98}\n220\t              suffix=\"%\"\n221\t              prefix={<SafetyCertificateOutlined />}\n222\t              valueStyle={{ color: colors.success[500] }}\n223\t            />\n224\t            <div style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>\n225\t              <Tag color=\"success\">运行正常</Tag>\n226\t            </div>\n227\t          </Card>\n228\t        </Col>\n229\t      </Row>\n230\t\n231\t      {/* 快速导航 */}\n232\t      <Card title=\"快速导航\">\n233\t        <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>\n234\t          <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={8}>\n235\t            <Card\n236\t              hoverable\n237\t              onClick={() => window.location.href = '/console/plugins'}\n238\t              style={{ height: '100%' }}\n239\t            >\n240\t              <Space direction=\"vertical\" size={8}>\n241\t                <AppstoreOutlined style={{ fontSize: 24, color: colors.primary[500] }} />\n242\t                <Text strong>插件管理</Text>\n243\t                <Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n244\t                  安装、配置和管理系统插件\n245\t                </Text>\n246\t              </Space>\n247\t            </Card>\n248\t          </Col>\n249\t          <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={8}>\n250\t            <Card\n251\t              hoverable\n252\t              onClick={() => window.location.href = '/console/settings'}\n253\t              style={{ height: '100%' }}\n254\t            >\n255\t              <Space direction=\"vertical\" size={8}>\n256\t                <SettingOutlined style={{ fontSize: 24, color: colors.purple[500] }} />\n257\t                <Text strong>系统配置</Text>\n258\t                <Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n259\t                  功能开关、特性管理、配置治理\n260\t                </Text>\n261\t              </Space>\n262\t            </Card>\n263\t          </Col>\n264\t          <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={8}>\n265\t            <Card\n266\t              hoverable\n267\t              onClick={() => window.location.href = '/console/users'}\n268\t              style={{ height: '100%' }}\n269\t            >\n270\t              <Space direction=\"vertical\" size={8}>\n271\t                <UserOutlined style={{ fontSize: 24, color: colors.info[500] }} />\n272\t                <Text strong>用户管理</Text>\n273\t                <Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n274\t                  用户、角色、权限管理\n275\t                </Text>\n276\t              </Space>\n277\t            </Card>\n278\t          </Col>\n279\t        </Row>\n280\t      </Card>\n281\t    </div>\n282\t  );\n283\t};\n284\t\n285\texport default {"parentUuid":"ef99ebf9-eba4-4fb2-ab2e-b98d02a170ea","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d96103ff-e4cf-48c5-b733-bda25a3fb2de","agentId":"afb517f0e340c4912","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_e3fc5252133044f9b6df7394","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Personal Workbench Page (个人工作台)\n3\t *\n4\t * Unified operational dashboard aggregating data from multiple services:\n5\t * - My Pipelines: recent runs, success rate, failure count\n6\t * - My Alerts: active alerts, critical count, acknowledge actions\n7\t * - My Tickets: active tickets, SLA countdown, priority tags\n8\t * - My Deployments: recent deployments, environment tags, status\n9\t *\n10\t * Features:\n11\t * - 4 summary stat cards at top\n12\t * - 2x2 grid of detail panels\n13\t * - Auto-refresh every 60 seconds\n14\t * - Loading skeleton, empty states\n15\t * - \"View more\" links to respective detail pages\n16\t */\n17\timport React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';\n18\timport {\n19\t  Card,\n20\t  Row,\n21\t  Col,\n22\t  Table,\n23\t  Tag,\n24\t  Badge,\n25\t  Button,\n26\t  Space,\n27\t  Typography,\n28\t  Statistic,\n29\t  Spin,\n30\t  Empty,\n31\t  Tooltip,\n32\t  Popconfirm,\n33\t  message,\n34\t} from 'antd';\n35\timport {\n36\t  ReloadOutlined,\n37\t  ClockCircleOutlined,\n38\t  WarningOutlined,\n39\t  OrderedListOutlined,\n40\t  CloudUploadOutlined,\n41\t  LinkOutlined,\n42\t  CheckCircleOutlined,\n43\t  CloseCircleOutlined,\n44\t  ThunderboltOutlined,\n45\t} from '@ant-design/icons';\n46\timport { colors, spacing } from '@/tokens';\n47\timport DataState from '@/components/DataState';\n48\timport {\n49\t  getWorkbenchData,\n50\t  type WorkbenchData,\n51\t  type PipelineRunSummary,\n52\t  type AlertSummary,\n53\t  type TicketSummary,\n54\t  type DeploymentSummary,\n55\t  acknowledgeAlert,\n56\t} from '@/api/workbench';\n57\timport dayjs from 'dayjs';\n58\timport relativeTime from 'dayjs/plugin/relativeTime';\n59\t\n60\tdayjs.extend(relativeTime);\n61\t\n62\tconst { Title, Text } = Typography;\n63\t\n64\t// ============================================================================\n65\t// Color constants\n66\t// ============================================================================\n67\t\n68\tconst COLORS = {\n69\t  success: colors.success[500],\n70\t  warning: colors.warning[500],\n71\t  error: colors.error[500],\n72\t  info: colors.primary[500],\n73\t  purple: colors.purple[500],\n74\t  cyan: colors.info[500],\n75\t};\n76\t\n77\t// ============================================================================\n78\t// Display helpers\n79\t// ============================================================================\n80\t\n81\t/** Status badge for pipeline runs and deployments */\n82\tconst StatusBadge: React.FC<{ status: string }> = ({ status }) => {\n83\t  const statusMap: Record<string, { color: string; text: string }> = {\n84\t    success: { color: 'success', text: '成功' },\n85\t    failed: { color: 'error', text: '失败' },\n86\t    running: { color: 'processing', text: '运行中' },\n87\t    pending: { color: 'default', text: '等待中' },\n88\t    cancelled: { color: 'default', text: '已取消' },\n89\t    deploying: { color: 'processing', text: '部署中' },\n90\t    rolled_back: { color: 'error', text: '已回滚' },\n91\t  };\n92\t  const { color, text } = statusMap[status] || { color: 'default', text: status };\n93\t  return <Badge status={color as any} text={text} />;\n94\t};\n95\t\n96\t/** Severity color mapping for alerts */\n97\tconst severityColor = (severity: string): string => {\n98\t  switch (severity) {\n99\t    case 'critical':\n100\t      return COLORS.error;\n101\t    case 'warning':\n102\t      return COLORS.warning;\n103\t    case 'info':\n104\t      return COLORS.info;\n105\t    default:\n106\t      return colors.neutral[400];\n107\t  }\n108\t};\n109\t\n110\t/** Severity display name */\n111\tconst severityName = (severity: string): string => {\n112\t  const names: Record<string, string> = {\n113\t    critical: '严重',\n114\t    warning: '警告',\n115\t    info: '信息',\n116\t  };\n117\t  return names[severity] || severity;\n118\t};\n119\t\n120\t/** Priority tag color */\n121\tconst priorityColor = (priority: string): string => {\n122\t  switch (priority) {\n123\t    case 'critical':\n124\t    case 'urgent':\n125\t      return COLORS.error;\n126\t    case 'high':\n127\t      return COLORS.warning;\n128\t    case 'medium':\n129\t      return COLORS.info;\n130\t    case 'low':\n131\t      return COLORS.success;\n132\t    default:\n133\t      return colors.neutral[400];\n134\t  }\n135\t};\n136\t\n137\t/** Priority display name */\n138\tconst priorityName = (priority: string): string => {\n139\t  const names: Record<string, string> = {\n140\t    critical: '紧急',\n141\t    urgent: '紧急',\n142\t    high: '高',\n143\t    medium: '中',\n144\t    low: '低',\n145\t  };\n146\t  return names[priority] || priority;\n147\t};\n148\t\n149\t/** Environment tag color */\n150\tconst envColor = (env: string): string => {\n151\t  switch (env) {\n152\t    case 'prod':\n153\t    case 'production':\n154\t      return COLORS.error;\n155\t    case 'staging':\n156\t    case 'pre-prod':\n157\t      return COLORS.warning;\n158\t    case 'dev':\n159\t    case 'development':\n160\t      return COLORS.info;\n161\t    default:\n162\t      return colors.neutral[400];\n163\t  }\n164\t};\n165\t\n166\t/** Environment display name */\n167\tconst envName = (env: string): string => {\n168\t  const names: Record<string, string> = {\n169\t    prod: '生产',\n170\t    production: '生产',\n171\t    staging: '预发',\n172\t    'pre-prod': '预发',\n173\t    dev: '开发',\n174\t    development: '开发',\n175\t  };\n176\t  return names[env] || env;\n177\t};\n178\t\n179\t/** Format duration from milliseconds to human readable */\n180\tconst formatDuration = (ms: number): string => {\n181\t  if (!ms) return '-';\n182\t  if (ms < 60000) return `${Math.round(ms / 1000)}s`;\n183\t  if (ms < 3600000) return `${Math.round(ms / 60000)}m`;\n184\t  const hours = Math.floor(ms / 3600000);\n185\t  const minutes = Math.round((ms % 3600000) / 60000);\n186\t  return `${hours}h ${minutes}m`;\n187\t};\n188\t\n189\t/** Format SLA remaining time */\n190\tconst formatSlaRemaining = (hours: number): { text: string; color: string } => {\n191\t  if (hours < 0) {\n192\t    return { text: `超时 ${Math.abs(hours)}h`, color: COLORS.error };\n193\t  }\n194\t  if (hours < 4) {\n195\t    return { text: `${hours}h`, color: COLORS.warning };\n196\t  }\n197\t  return { text: `${hours}h`, color: 'inherit' };\n198\t};\n199\t\n200\t// ============================================================================\n201\t// Main Component\n202\t// ============================================================================\n203\t\n204\tconst WorkbenchPage: React.FC = () => {\n205\t  const [data, setData] = useState<WorkbenchData | null>(null);\n206\t  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n207\t  const [error, setError] = useState<Error | null>(null);\n208\t  const [lastRefresh, setLastRefresh] = useState<Date>(new Date());\n209\t\n210\t  // Fetch workbench data\n211\t  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {\n212\t    try {\n213\t      setLoading(true);\n214\t      setError(null);\n215\t      const result = await getWorkbenchData();\n216\t      setData(result);\n217\t      setLastRefresh(new Date());\n218\t    } catch (err) {\n219\t      setError(err instanceof Error ? err : new Error('Failed to fetch workbench data'));\n220\t    } finally {\n221\t      setLoading(false);\n222\t    }\n223\t  }, []);\n224\t\n225\t  // Initial fetch + auto-refresh every 60 seconds\n226\t  useEffect(() => {\n227\t    fetchData();\n228\t    const timer = setInterval(fetchData, 60000);\n229\t    return () => clearInterval(timer);\n230\t  }, [fetchData]);\n231\t\n232\t  // Acknowledge an alert\n233\t  const handleAcknowledge = async (alertId: string) => {\n234\t    try {\n235\t      await acknowledgeAlert(alertId);\n236\t      message.success('告警已确认');\n237\t      // Refresh data after acknowledge\n238\t      fetchData();\n239\t    } catch {\n240\t      message.error('确认告警失败');\n241\t    }\n242\t  };\n243\t\n244\t  // ============================================================================\n245\t  // Loading skeleton\n246\t  // ============================================================================\n247\t\n248\t  if (loading && !data) {\n249\t    return (\n250\t      <div style={{ padding: 0 }}>\n251\t        <div style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>\n252\t          <Title level={2} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>\n253\t            <ThunderboltOutlined style={{ marginRight: 12, color: colors.primary[500] }} />\n254\t            个人工作台\n255\t          </Title>\n256\t        </div>\n257\t        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '80px 0' }}>\n258\t          <Spin size=\"large\" tip=\"加载工作台数据...\" />\n259\t        </div>\n260\t      </div>\n261\t    );\n262\t  }\n263\t\n264\t  // ============================================================================\n265\t  // Error state\n266\t  // ============================================================================\n267\t\n268\t  if (error && !data) {\n269\t    return (\n270\t      <DataState loading={false} error={error} retry={fetchData} />\n271\t    );\n272\t  }\n273\t\n274\t  // ============================================================================\n275\t  // Empty state\n276\t  // ============================================================================\n277\t\n278\t  const isEmpty =\n279\t    !data ||\n280\t    (data.myPipelines.recentRuns.length === 0 &&\n281\t      data.myAlerts.recent.length === 0 &&\n282\t      data.myTickets.recent.length === 0 &&\n283\t      data.myDeployments.recent.length === 0);\n284\t\n285\t  if (isEmpty && !loading) {\n286\t    return );\n300\t  }\n301\t\n302\t  // Safety fallback\n303\t  if (!data) return null;\n304\t\n305\t  // ============================================================================\n306\t  // Render\n307\t     </Tooltip>\n343\t      ),\n344\t    },\n345\t  ];\n346\t\n347\t  // Alert columns\n348\t  const alertColumns = [\n349\t    {\n350\t      title: '告警内容',\n351\t      dataIndex: 'message',\n352\t </Space>\n361\t      ),\n362\t    },\n363\t    {\n364\t      title: '时间',\n365\t      dataIndex: 'createdAt',\n366\t      key: 'createdAt',\n367\t      width: 120,\n368\t      render: (time: string) => dayjs(time).fromNow(),\n369\t    },\n370\t    {\n371\t      title: '操作',\n372\t      key: 'action',\n373\t      width: 100,\n374\t      render: (_: unknown, record: AlertSummary) =>\n375\t        !record.acknowledged ? </Button>\n380\t          </Popconfirm>\n381\t        ) : (\n382\t          <Tag icon={<CheckCircleOutlined />} color=\"success\" style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n383\t            已确认\n384\t          </Tag>\n385\t        ),\n386\t    },\n387\t  ];\n388\t\n389\t  // Ticket columns\n390\t  const ticketColumns = [\n391\t    {\n392\t      title: '工单标题',\n393\t      dataIndex: 'title',\n394\t      key: {\n401\t      title: '优先级',\n402\t      dataIndex: 'priority',\n403\t      key: 'priority',\n404\t      width: 80,\n405\t      render: (priority: string) => (\n406\t        <Tag ];\n426\t\n427\t  // Deployment columns\n428\t  const deploymentColumns = [\n429\t    {\n430\t      title: '版本',\n431\t      dataIndex: 'version',\n432\t      key: 'version',\n433\t      ellipsis:  width: 100,\n441\t      render: (env: string) => (\n442\t        <Tag color={envColor(env)} style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>\n443\t          {envName(env)}\n444\t        </Tag>\n445\t      ),\n446\t    },\n447\t    {\n448\t      title: '状态',\n449\t      dataIndex: 'status',\n450\t      key: 'status',\n451\t      width: 120,\n452\t      render: (status: string) => <StatusBadge status={status} <div style={{ padding: 0 }}>\n469\t      {/* Page header */}\n470\t      <div style={{ marginBottom: 24, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>\n471\t        <div>\n472\t          <Title level={2} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>\n473\t            <ThunderboltOutlined style={{ marginRight: 12, color: colors.primary[500] }} />\n474\t            个人工作台\n475\t />\n480\t            最后刷新: {dayjs(lastRefresh).format('HH:mm:ss')}\n481\t          </Text>\n482\t        </div>\n483\t        <Button\n484\t          icon={<ReloadOutlined spin={loading} />}\n485\t   <Col xs={12} sm={12} md={6}>\n497\t            <Card size=\"small\">\n498\t              <Statistic\n499\t                title=\"流水线成功率\"\n500\t                value={data.myPipelines.successRate ============================================================================\n82\t\n83\t/**\n84\t * Fetch unified workbench data from aggregated endpoint.\n85\t * Falls back to individual API calls if endpoint not available.\n86\t */\n87\texport async function getWorkbenchData(): Promise<WorkbenchData> {\n88\t  try {\n89\t    const response = await api.get('/v1/workbench');\n90\t    const body = unexpected format');\n95\t  } catch {\n96\t    // Unified endpoint not available, use fallback\n97\t    return getWorkbenchFallback();\n98\t  }\n99\t}\n100\t\n101\t// ============================================================================\n104\t\n105\t/**\n106\t * Fallback implementation that calls individual APIs in parallel\n107\t * and assembles the workbench data from their responses.\n108\t */\n109\texport async function getWorkbenchFallback(): Promise<WorkbenchData> {\n110\t  const [pipelines, alerts, tickets, deployments] = await Promise.allSettled([\n111\t    api.get('/v1/pipeline-runs?limit=5&status=all'),\n112\t    api.get('/v1/alerts?limit=5&status=active'),\n113\t    api.get('/v1/tickets?limit=5&status=active'),\n114\t    api.get('/v1/deployments?limit=5'),\n115\t  ]);\n116\t\n117\t  // Parse pipeline runs\n118\t  const pipelineItems =\n119\t    pipelines.status === 'fulfilled'\n120\t      ? (pipelines.value.data?.items || pipelines.value.data?.data || [])\n121\t      : [];\n122\t\n123\t  // Parse alerts\n124\t  const alertItems =\n125\t    alerts.status === 'fulfilled'\n126\t      ? (alerts.value.data?.items || alerts.value.data?.data || [])\n127\t      : [];\n128\t\n129\t  // Parse tickets\n130\t  const ticketItems =\n131\t    tickets.status === 'fulfilled'\n132\t      ? (tickets.value.data?.items || tickets.value.data?.data || [])\n133\t      : [];\n134\t\n135\t  // Parse deployments\n136\t  const deploymentItems =\n137\t    deployments.status === 'fulfilled'\n138\t      ? (deployments.value.data?.items || deployments.value.data?.data || [])\n139\t      : [];\n140\t\n141\t  return {\n142\t    myPipelines: {\n143\t      recentRuns: pipelineItems.map((run: any) => ({\n144\t        id: run.id || '',\n145\t        name: run.pipelineName || run.name || 'Unknown Pipeline',\n146\t        status: run.status || 'unknown',\n147\t        createdAt: run.createdAt || run.startedAt || '',\n148\t        duration: run.duration || run.durationMs || 0,\n149\t      })),\n150\t      successRate: 0,\n151\t      totalRuns24h: 0,\n152\t      failedRuns: pipelineItems.filter((r: any) => r.status === 'failed').length,\n153\t    },\n154\t    myAlerts: {\n155\t      unread: alertItems.length,\n156\t      critical: alertItems.filter((a: any) => a.severity === 'critical').length,\n157\t      recent: alertItems.map((alert: any) => ({\n158\t        id: alert.id || '',\n159\t        severity: alert.severity || 'info',\n160\t        message: alert.message || alert.description || '',\n161\t        createdAt: alert.createdAt || '',\n162\t        acknowledged: alert.status === 'acknowledged' || alert.acknowledged || false,\n163\t      })),\n164\t    },\n165\t    myTickets: {\n166\t      active: ticketItems.length,\n167\t      overdue: ticketItems.filter((t: any) => t.isOverdue || t.slaRemainingHours < 0).length,\n168\t      recent: ticketItems.map((ticket: any) => ({\n169\t        id: ticket.id || ticket.ticketId || '',\n170\t        title: ticket.title || '',\n171\t        priority: ticket.priority || 'medium',\n172\t        status: ticket.status || 'pending',\n173\t        slaRemaining: ticket.slaRemainingHours ?? ticket.slaRemaining ?? 0,\n174\t      })),\n175\t    },\n176\t    myDeployments: {\n177\t      recent: deploymentItems.map((dep: any) => ({\n178\t        id: dep.id || '',\n179\t        environment: dep.environment || 'unknown',\n180\t        status: dep.status || 'unknown',\n181\t        version: dep.version || dep.appVersion || '',\n182\t        deployedAt: dep.deployedAt || dep.createdAt || '',\n183\t      })),\n184\t      successRate: 0,\n185\t    },\n186\t  };\n187\t}\n188\t\n189\t// ============================================================================\n190\t// Individual API helpers (for granular calls)\n191\t// ============================================================================\n192\t\n193\t/** Fetch my recent pipeline runs */\n194\texport function getMyPipelineRuns(limit = 5) {\n195\t  return api.get('/v1/pipeline-runs', { params: { limit, status: 'all' } });\n196\t}\n197\t\n198\t/** Fetch my active alerts */\n199\texport function getMyAlerts(limit = 5) {\n200\t  return api.get('/v1/alerts', { params: { limit, status: 'active' } });\n201\t}\n202\t\n203\t/** Fetch my active tickets */\n204\texport function getMyTickets(limit = 5) {\n205\t  return api.get('/v1/tickets', { params: { limit, status: 'active' } });\n206\t}\n207\t\n208\t/** Fetch my recent deployments */\n209\texport function getMyDeployments(limit = 5) {\n210\t  return api.get('/v1/deployments', { params: { limit } });\n211\t}\n212\t\n213\t/** Acknowledge an alert */\n214\texport function acknowledgeAlert(alertId: string) {\n215\t  return api.post(`/v1/alerts/${alertId}/acknowledge`);\n216\t}\n217\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. userId?: string; userGroups?: string[] }\n81\t) {\n82\t  return api.post<FlagEvaluationResult>(`/v1/feature-flags/${id}/evaluate`, context);\n83\t}\n84\t\n85\texport async function getFeatureFlagStats() {\n86\t  return api.get<FeatureFlagStats>('/v1/feature-flags/stats');\n87\t}\n88\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. uptime: number; metricsCount: number }>('/v1/monitoring/health');\n64\t}\n65\t\n66\t// ==================== Metrics ====================\n67\t\n68\texport function getMetrics(params?: { tags?: string }) {\n69\t  return api.get<Metric[]>('/v1/monitoring/metrics', { params });\n70\t}\n71\t\n72\texport function recordMetric(data: { name: string; value: number; tags?: Record<string, string> }) {\n73\t  return api.post<{ recorded: boolean }>('/v1/monitoring/metrics', data);\n74\t}\n75\t\n76\texport function registerMetric(data: {\n77\t  name: string;\n78\t  type: string;\n79\t  unit: string;\n80\t  tags?: Record<string, string>;\n81\t}) {\n82\t  return api.post<{ registered: boolean }>('/v1/monitoring/metrics/register', data);\n83\t}\n84\t\n85\texport function getMetricSeries(\n86\t  name: string,\n87\t  params?: { from?: string; to?: string; step?: string }\n88\t) {\n89\t  return api.get<{ points: Array<{ timestamp: string; value: number }> }>(\n90\t    `/v1/monitoring/metrics/${name}/series`,\n91\t    { params }\n92\t  );\n93\t}\n94\t\n95\texport function getMetricSummary(name: string, params?: { window?: string }) {\n96\t  return api.get<{ avg: number; min: number; max: number; p95: number; count: number }>(\n97\t    `/v1/monitoring/metrics/${name}/summary`,\n98\t    { params }\n99\t  );\n100\t}\n101\t\n102\t// ==================== Alert Rules ====================\n103\t\n104\texport function getAlertRules() {\n105\t  return api.get<AlertRule[]>('/v1/monitoring/rules');\n106\t}\n107\t\n108\texport function createAlertRule(data: Omit<AlertRule, 'id'>) {\n109\t  return api.post<AlertRule>('/v1/monitoring/rules', data);\n110\t}\n111\t\n112\texport function updateAlertRule(id: string, data: Partial<AlertRule>) {\n113\t  return api.put<AlertRule>(`/v1/monitoring/rules/${id}`, data);\n114\t}\n115\t\n116\texport function deleteAlertRule(id: string) {\n117\t  return api.delete<{ deleted: boolean }>(`/v1/monitoring/rules/${id}`);\n118\t}\n119\t\n120\texport function toggleAlertRule(id: string) {\n121\t  return api.patch<{ enabled: boolean }>(`/v1/monitoring/rules/${id}/toggle`);\n122\t}\n123\t\n124\texport function evaluateAlertRule(data: { ruleId: string }) {\n125\t  return api.post<{ triggered: boolean; value: number }>(`/v1/monitoring/rules/evaluate`, data);\n126\t}\n127\t\n128\texport function suppressAlertRule(id: string) {\n129\t  return api.post<{ suppressed: boolean }>(`/v1/monitoring/rules/${id}/suppress`);\n130\t}\n131\t\n132\texport function unsuppressAlertRule(id: string) {\n133\t  return api.post<{ suppressed: boolean }>(`/v1/monitoring/rules/${id}/unsuppress`);\n134\t}\n135\t\n136\t// ==================== Alerts ====================\n137\t\n138\texport function getAlerts(params?: {\n139\t  status?: string;\n140\t  severity?: string;\n141\t  from?: string;\n142\t  to?: string;\n143\t}) {\n144\t  return api.get<Alert[]>('/v1/monitoring/alerts', { params });\n145\t}\n146\t\n147\texport function getActiveAlerts() {\n148\t  return api.get<Alert[]>('/v1/monitoring/alerts/active');\n149\t}\n150\t\n151\texport function getAlert(id: string) {\n152\t  return api.get<Alert>(`/v1/monitoring/alerts/${id}`);\n153\t}\n154\t\n155\texport function acknowledgeAlert(id: string, data?: { note?: string }) {\n156\t  return api.post<{ acknowledged: boolean }>(`/v1/monitoring/alerts/${id}/acknowledge`, data);\n157\t}\n158\t\n159\texport function resolveAlert(id: string) {\n160\t  return api.post<{ resolved: boolean }>(`/v1/monitoring/alerts/${id}/resolve`);\n161\t}\n162\t\n163\texport function escalateAlert(id: string, data: { reason: string; target?: string }) {\n164\t  return api.post<{ escalated: boolean }>(`/v1/monitoring/alerts/${id}/escalate`, data);\n165\t}\n166\t\n167\t// ==================== Notification Channels ====================\n168\t\n169\texport function getChannels() {\n170\t  return api.get<NotificationChannel[]>('/v1/monitoring/channels');\n171\t}\n172\t\n173\texport function createChannel(data: Omit<NotificationChannel, 'id'>) {\n174\t  return api.post<NotificationChannel>('/v1/monitoring/channels', data);\n175\t}\n176\t\n177\texport function toggleChannel(id: string) {\n178\t  return api.patch<{ enabled: boolean }>(`/v1/monitoring/channels/${id}/toggle`);\n179\t}\n180\t\n181\t// ==================== Escalation Policies ====================\n182\t\n183\texport function getEscalationPolicies() {\n184\t  return api.get<EscalationPolicy[]>('/v1/monitoring/escalation');\n185\t}\n186\t\n187\texport function createEscalationPolicy(data: Omit<EscalationPolicy, 'id'>) {\n188\t  return api.post<EscalationPolicy>('/v1/monitoring/escalation', data);\n189\t}\n190\t\n191\t// ==================== Notifications ====================\n192\t\n193\texport function getNotifications(params?: { channel?: string; from?: string; to?: string }) {\n194\t  return api.get<Array<{ id: string; channelId: string; sentAt: string; status: string }>>(\n195\t    '/v1/monitoring/notifications',\n196\t    { params }\n197\t  );\n198\t}\n199\t\n200\t// ==================== Dashboard & Anomalies ====================\n201\t\n202\texport function getDashboardData() {\n203\t  return api.get<{\n204\t    alerts: { total: number; active: number; resolved: number };\n205\t    rules: { total: number; enabled: number };\n206\t    metrics: { total: number; rate: number };\n207\t    channels: { total: number; active: number };\n208\t  }>('/v1/monitoring/dashboard');\n209\t}\n210\t\n211\texport function getAnomalySummary(params?: { window?: string }) {\n212\t  return api.get<{\n213\t    anomalies: Array<{ metric: string; time: string; severity: string }>;\n214\t    totalCount: number;\n215\t  }>('/v1/monitoring/anomalies/summary', { params });\n216\t}\n217\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. {"parentUuid":"f365b225-62db-4719-b786-f274b8591d4f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"afb517f0e340c4912","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_99fbd6e40cb14ab289190481","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Configuration Management API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * Provides endpoints for config CRUD, GitOps sync, approval workflows,\n5\t * and config diff/comparison operations.\n6\t *\n7\t * Prefix: /api/v1/config\n8\t */\n9\t\n10\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n11\timport { ConfigRepository } from '../services/config-mgmt/ConfigRepository';\n12\timport { ConfigService } from '../services/config-mgmt/ConfigService';\n13\timport { GitOpsService } from '../services/config-mgmt/GitOpsService';\n14\timport { ConfigApprovalService } from '../services/config-mgmt/ConfigApprovalService';\n15\timport { ConfigDiffService } from '../services/config-mgmt/ConfigDiffService';\n16\timport { ConfigController } from './controllers/ConfigController';\n17\timport { DatabasePool } from '../services/database';\n18\timport { RedisCache } from '../services/redis-cache';\n19\timport { CacheService } from '../services/cache/CacheService';\n20\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n21\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n22\t\n23\texport interface ConfigRoutesOptions {\n24\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n25\t  redis?: RedisCache;\n26\t}\n27\t\n28\texport default async function configRoutes(\n29\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n30\t  options: ConfigRoutesOptions\n31\t): Promise<void> {\n32\t  if (!options.database) {\n33\t    console.warn('[ConfigRoutes] No database pool provided, config routes will not be functional');\n34\t    return;\n35\t  }\n36\t  // Initialize repository with PostgreSQL connection\n37\t  const configRepo = new ConfigRepository(options.database);\n38\t\n39\t  // Initialize services - all depend on ConfigService which now uses the repository\n40\t  const cache = new CacheService(options.redis || null, 120);\n41\t  const configService = new ConfigService(configRepo, cache);\n42\t  const gitOpsService = new GitOpsService({ configService });\n43\t  const approvalService = new ConfigApprovalService({ configService });\n44\t  const diffService = new ConfigDiffService({ configService });\n45\t\n46\t  // Initialize controller\n47\t  const configController = new ConfigController(\n48\t    configService,\n49\t    gitOpsService,\n50\t    approvalService,\n51\t    diffService\n52\t  );\n53\t\n54\t  // ==================== Config CRUD ====================\n55\t\n56\t  // POST /configs - Create configuration item\n57\t  app.post('/configs', {\n58\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'write' })],\n59\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n60\t    return configController.create(request, reply);\n61\t  });\n62\t\n63\t  // GET /configs - List configurations\n64\t  app.get('/configs', {\n65\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n66\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n67\t    return configController.list(request, reply);\n68\t  });\n69\t\n70\t  // GET /configs/:configId - Get config detail\n71\t  app.get(\n72\t    '/configs/:configId',\n73\t    {\n74\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n75\t    },\n76\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n77\t      return configController.getById(request, reply);\n78\t    }\n79\t  );\n80\t\n81\t  // PUT /configs/:configId - Update configuration\n82\t  app.put(\n83\t    '/configs/:configId',\n84\t    {\n85\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'write' })],\n86\t    },\n87\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n88\t      return configController.update(request, reply);\n89\t    }\n90\t  );\n91\t\n92\t  // DELETE /configs/:configId - Delete configuration (soft delete)\n93\t  app.delete(\n94\t    '/configs/:configId',\n95\t    {\n96\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'delete' })],\n97\t    },\n98\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n99\t      return configController.delete(request, reply);\n100\t    }\n101\t  );\n102\t\n103\t  // GET /configs/:configId/versions - Get config version history\n104\t  app.get(\n105\t    '/configs/:configId/versions',\n106\t    {\n107\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n108\t    },\n109\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n110\t      return configController.getVersions(request, reply);\n111\t    }\n112\t  );\n113\t\n114\t  // POST /configs/:configId/rollback - Rollback to a specific version\n115\t  app.post(\n116\t    '/configs/:configId/rollback',\n117\t    {\n118\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'manage' })],\n119\t    },\n120\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n121\t      return configController.rollback(request, reply);\n122\t    }\n123\t  );\n124\t\n125\t  // POST /configs/:configId/clone - Clone config to another environment\n126\t  app.post(\n127\t    '/configs/:configId/clone',\n128\t    {\n129\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'write' })],\n130\t    },\n131\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n132\t      return configController.clone(request, reply);\n133\t    }\n134\t  );\n135\t\n136\t  // ==================== GitOps ====================\n137\t\n138\t  // POST /gitops - Enable GitOps synchronization\n139\t  app.post('/gitops', {\n140\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'manage' })],\n141\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n142\t    return configController.enableGitOps(request, reply);\n143\t  });\n144\t\n145\t  // GET /gitops - List GitOps configurations\n146\t  app.get('/gitops', {\n147\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n148\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n149\t    return configController.listGitOpsConfigs(request, reply);\n150\t  });\n151\t\n152\t  // POST /gitops/:gitOpsConfigId/sync - Trigger manual sync\n153\t  app.post(\n154\t    '/gitops/:gitOpsConfigId/sync',\n155\t    {\n156\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'execute' })],\n157\t    },\n158\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n159\t      return configController.syncFromGit(request, reply);\n160\t    }\n161\t  );\n162\t\n163\t  // POST /gitops/:gitOpsConfigId/disable - Disable GitOps\n164\t  app.post(\n165\t    '/gitops/:gitOpsConfigId/disable',\n166\t    {\n167\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'manage' })],\n168\t    },\n169\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n170\t      return configController.disableGitOps(request, reply);\n171\t    }\n172\t  );\n173\t\n174\t  // GET /gitops/drift - Detect configuration drift\n175\t  app.get(\n176\t    '/gitops/drift',\n177\t    {\n178\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n179\t    },\n180\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n181\t      return configController.detectDrift(request, reply);\n182\t    }\n183\t  );\n184\t\n185\t  // GET /gitops/sync-status - Get sync status history\n186\t  app.get(\n187\t    '/gitops/sync-status',\n188\t    {\n189\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n190\t    },\n191\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n192\t      return configController.getSyncStatus(request, reply);\n193\t    }\n194\t  );\n195\t\n196\t  // ==================== Approval Workflow ====================\n197\t\n198\t  // POST /change-requests - Create a config change request\n199\t  app.post(\n200\t    '/change-requests',\n201\t    {\n202\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'write' })],\n203\t    },\n204\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n205\t      return configController.createChangeRequest(request, reply);\n206\t    }\n207\t  );\n208\t\n209\t  // GET /change-requests - List change requests\n210\t  app.get(\n211\t    '/change-requests',\n212\t    {\n213\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n214\t    },\n215\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n216\t      return configController.listChangeRequests(request, reply);\n217\t    }\n218\t  );\n219\t\n220\t  // GET /change-requests/:changeRequestId - Get change request detail\n221\t  app.get(\n222\t    '/change-requests/:changeRequestId',\n223\t    {\n224\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n225\t    },\n226\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n227\t      return configController.getChangeRequest(request, reply);\n228\t    }\n229\t  );\n230\t\n231\t  // POST /change-requests/:changeRequestId/approve - Approve change request\n232\t  app.post(\n233\t    '/change-requests/:changeRequestId/approve',\n234\t    {\n235\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'approve' })],\n236\t    },\n237\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n238\t      return configController.approveChange(request, reply);\n239\t    }\n240\t  );\n241\t\n242\t  // POST /change-requests/:changeRequestId/reject - Reject change request\n243\t  app.post(\n244\t    '/change-requests/:changeRequestId/reject',\n245\t    {\n246\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'approve' })],\n247\t    },\n248\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n249\t      return configController.rejectChange(request, reply);\n250\t    }\n251\t  );\n252\t\n253\t  // GET /configs/:configId/audit - Get audit trail for a config\n254\t  app.get(\n255\t    '/configs/:configId/audit',\n256\t    {\n257\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n258\t    },\n259\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n260\t      return configController.getAuditTrail(request, reply);\n261\t    }\n262\t  );\n263\t\n264\t  // ==================== Diff & Comparison ====================\n265\t\n266\t  // GET /diff/:sourceEnv/:targetEnv - Compare environments\n267\t  app.get(\n268\t    '/diff/:sourceEnv/:targetEnv',\n269\t    {\n270\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n271\t    },\n272\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n273\t      return configController.compareEnvironments(request, reply);\n274\t    }\n275\t  );\n276\t\n277\t  // GET /configs/:configId/versions/diff - Compare config versions\n278\t  app.get(\n279\t    '/configs/:configId/versions/diff',\n280\t    {\n281\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n282\t    },\n283\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n284\t      return configController.compareVersions(request, reply);\n285\t    }\n286\t  );\n287\t\n288\t  // GET /diff/report - Generate comprehensive diff report\n289\t  app.get(\n290\t    '/diff/report',\n291\t    {\n292\t      onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'config', action: 'read' })],\n293\t    },\n294\t    async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n295\t      return configController.getDiffReport(request, reply);\n296\t    }\n297\t  );\n298\t}\n299\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code.
