{"parentUuid":"9dbda97f-793c-4402-acc8-16e655cf1208","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"afb517f0e340c4912","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_412c3f2ceb9d43fdb325a6ab","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// orion-platform-service/src/api/plugin-routes.ts\n2\t// Plugin Management API Routes (enhanced plugin system)\n3\t\n4\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n5\timport { PluginManagerService } from '../services/plugin-manager-service';\n6\timport { PluginExecutorService, registerExecutorForShutdown } from '../services/plugin-executor-service';\n7\timport { ExecutionTimelineService, registerTimelineForShutdown } from '../services/observability/ExecutionTimelineService';\n8\timport { AIDiagnosisService } from '../services/ai/AIDiagnosisService';\n9\timport { DebugController } from '../engine/DebugController';\n10\timport { PostgresPluginAuditLogRepository } from '../repositories/PluginAuditLogRepository';\n11\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n12\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n13\timport pino from 'pino';\n14\t\n15\tconst logger = pino({ level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL || 'info' });\n16\t\n17\texport interface PluginEnhancedRoutesOptions {\n18\t  database?: any;\n19\t  /** Shared PluginManagerService instance (created once, shared across routes) */\n20\t  pluginManager?: PluginManagerService;\n21\t}\n22\t\n23\texport default async function pluginEnhancedRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, options?: PluginEnhancedRoutesOptions): Promise<void> {\n24\t  // Use shared instance or create one\n25\t  const pluginManager = options?.pluginManager || new PluginManagerService();\n26\t  const pluginExecutor = new PluginExecutorService({\n27\t    pluginManager,\n28\t  });\n29\t\n30\t  // Register for graceful shutdown\n31\t  registerExecutorForShutdown(pluginExecutor);\n32\t  const timelineService = new ExecutionTimelineService();\n33\t  registerTimelineForShutdown(timelineService);\n34\t  const aiDiagnosis = new AIDiagnosisService();\n35\t  const auditLogRepo = options?.database ? new PostgresPluginAuditLogRepository(options.database) : undefined;\n36\t  const debugController = DebugController.getInstance();\n37\t\n38\t  // GET /healthz - Health check for plugin subsystem\n39\t  app.get('/healthz', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n40\t    const activeCount = pluginExecutor.getActiveExecutionCount();\n41\t    const isHealthy = activeCount < 50; // Below max concurrent threshold\n42\t    return {\n43\t      status: isHealthy ? 'healthy' : 'degraded',\n44\t      activeExecutions: activeCount,\n45\t    };\n46\t  });\n47\t\n48\t  // GET / - List all installed plugins\n49\t  app.get('/', {\n50\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'read' })],\n51\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n52\t    const tenantId = (request as any).tenantId;\n53\t    const plugins = await pluginManager.listAvailablePlugins();\n54\t    return { plugins, tenantId };\n55\t  });\n56\t\n57\t  // GET /:pluginId - Get plugin details\n58\t  app.get('/:pluginId', {\n59\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'read' })],\n60\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n61\t    const { pluginId } = request.params as { pluginId: string };\n62\t    try {\n63\t      const plugin = await pluginManager.getPluginDetails(pluginId);\n64\t      return { plugin };\n65\t    } catch (error) {\n66\t      return reply.code(404).send({ error: `Plugin ${pluginId} not found` });\n67\t    }\n68\t  });\n69\t\n70\t  // POST /:pluginId/install - Install a plugin\n71\t  app.post('/:pluginId/install', {\n72\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'write' })],\n73\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n74\t    const { pluginId } = request.params as { pluginId: string };\n75\t    const body = request.body as any;\n76\t    const userId = (request as any).userId;\n77\t    try {\n78\t      const version = body?.version || 'latest';\n79\t      const config = body?.config;\n80\t      const result = await pluginManager.installPlugin(pluginId, version, config);\n81\t      return { pluginId, action: 'install', version, userId };\n82\t    } catch (error) {\n83\t      return reply.code(400).send({ error: `Failed to install plugin ${pluginId}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });\n84\t    }\n85\t  });\n86\t\n87\t  // POST /:pluginId/enable - Enable a plugin\n88\t  app.post('/:pluginId/enable', {\n89\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'write' })],\n90\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n91\t    const { pluginId } = request.params as { pluginId: string };\n92\t    try {\n93\t      const plugin = await pluginManager.activatePlugin(pluginId);\n94\t      return { pluginId, action: 'enable', status: plugin.state };\n95\t    } catch (error) {\n96\t      return reply.code(400).send({ error: `Failed to enable plugin ${pluginId}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });\n97\t    }\n98\t  });\n99\t\n100\t  // POST /:pluginId/disable - Disable a plugin\n101\t  app.post('/:pluginId/disable', {\n102\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'write' })],\n103\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n104\t    const { pluginId } = request.params as { pluginId: string };\n105\t    try {\n106\t      const plugin = await pluginManager.deactivatePlugin(pluginId);\n107\t      return { pluginId, action: 'disable', status: plugin.state };\n108\t    } catch (error) {\n109\t      return reply.code(400).send({ error: `Failed to disable plugin ${pluginId}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });\n110\t    }\n111\t  });\n112\t\n113\t  // DELETE /:pluginId - Uninstall a plugin\n114\t  app.delete('/:pluginId', {\n115\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'delete' })],\n116\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n117\t    const { pluginId } = request.params as { pluginId: string };\n118\t    try {\n119\t      await pluginManager.uninstallPlugin(pluginId);\n120\t      return { pluginId, action: 'uninstall', status: 'uninstalled' };\n121\t    } catch (error) {\n122\t      return reply.code(400).send({ error: `Failed to uninstall plugin ${pluginId}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });\n123\t    }\n124\t  });\n125\t\n126\t  // GET /audit - Get audit logs\n127\t  app.get('/audit', {\n128\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'read' })],\n129\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n130\t    const query = request.query as any;\n131\t    const tenantId = (request as any).tenantId;\n132\t    const limit = query?.limit || 50;\n133\t\n134\t    if (auditLogRepo && tenantId) {\n135\t      const logs = await auditLogRepo.findByTenantId(tenantId, limit);\n136\t      return { logs: logs.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, taskId: l.taskId, pluginId: l.pluginId, action: l.action, outcome: l.outcome, createdAt: l.createdAt })), tenantId, limit };\n137\t    }\n138\t\n139\t    return { logs: [], tenantId, limit, status: 'not_implemented' };\n140\t  });\n141\t\n142\t  // GET /audit/:taskId/trail - Get task audit trail\n143\t  app.get('/audit/:taskId/trail', {\n144\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'read' })],\n145\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n146\t    const { taskId } = request.params as { taskId: string };\n147\t\n148\t    if (auditLogRepo) {\n149\t      const logs = await auditLogRepo.findByTaskId(taskId);\n150\t      return { taskId, logs: logs.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, action: l.action, outcome: l.outcome, durationMs: l.durationMs, createdAt: l.createdAt })) };\n151\t    }\n152\t\n153\t    return { taskId, logs: [], status: 'not_implemented' };\n154\t  });\n155\t\n156\t  // GET /:runId/timeline - Get execution timeline\n157\t  app.get('/:runId/timeline', {\n158\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'read' })],\n159\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n160\t    const { runId } = request.params as { runId: string };\n161\t    const replayData = await timelineService.getReplayData(runId);\n162\t    return replayData;\n163\t  });\n164\t\n165\t  // POST /:runId/debug/pause - Pause for debug\n166\t  app.post('/:runId/debug/pause', {\n167\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'manage' })],\n168\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n169\t    const { runId } = request.params as { runId: string };\n170\t    try {\n171\t      const state = await debugController.pause(runId);\n172\t      return { runId, status: 'paused', debugState: state };\n173\t    } catch (error) {\n174\t      return reply.code(500).send({ error: `Failed to pause: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });\n175\t    }\n176\t  });\n177\t\n178\t  // POST /:runId/debug/resume - Resume execution\n179\t  app.post('/:runId/debug/resume', {\n180\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'manage' })],\n181\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n182\t    const { runId } = request.params as { runId: string };\n183\t    try {\n184\t      await debugController.resume(runId);\n185\t      return { runId, status: 'resumed' };\n186\t    } catch (error) {\n187\t      return reply.code(400).send({ error: `Failed to resume: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });\n188\t    }\n189\t  });\n190\t\n191\t  // POST /:runId/debug/step - Single step execution\n192\t  app.post('/:runId/debug/step', {\n193\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'manage' })],\n194\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n195\t    const { runId } = request.params as { runId: string };\n196\t    try {\n197\t      const state = await debugController.step(runId);\n198\t      return { runId, status: 'stepping', debugState: state };\n199\t    } catch (error) {\n200\t      return reply.code(400).send({ error: `Failed to step: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}` });\n201\t    }\n202\t  });\n203\t\n204\t  // GET /:runId/debug/state - Get debug state\n205\t  app.get('/:runId/debug/state', {\n206\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'read' })],\n207\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n208\t    const { runId } = request.params as { runId: string };\n209\t    const state = debugController.getState(runId);\n210\t    if (!state) {\n211\t      return reply.code(404).send({ error: `No debug state found for run ${runId}` });\n212\t    }\n213\t    return state;\n214\t  });\n215\t\n216\t  // POST /ai-diagnose - AI error diagnosis\n217\t  app.post('/ai-diagnose', {\n218\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'plugin', action: 'execute' })],\n219\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n220\t    const body = request.body as any;\n221\t    if (!body?.context?.taskId || !body?.context?.pluginId || !body?.context?.errorMessage) {\n222\t      return reply.code(400).send({ error: 'Missing required context fields: taskId, pluginId, errorMessage' });\n223\t    }\n224\t    const result = await aiDiagnosis.diagnose(body.context);\n225\t    return result;\n226\t  });\n227\t\n228\t  logger.info('Plugin enhanced routes registered');\n229\t}\n230\t\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"426e1618-6faa-48f9-b297-1ad1f2f64210","timestamp":"2026-05-22T08:06:59.443Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9dbda97f-793c-4402-acc8-16e655cf1208","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}
{"parentUuid":"c9e0489e-996e-4a68-9ad7-49839ef411aa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"afb517f0e340c4912","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_ea1cf5cacce5417296bb23da","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * WorkbenchService - Aggregated personal workbench for engineers\n3\t *\n4\t * Combines pipeline, alert, ticket, and deployment data into a single\n5\t * personalized view for the \"个人工作台\" frontend page.\n6\t */\n7\t\n8\timport { DatabasePool } from '../database';\n9\t\n10\texport interface WorkbenchPipelineData {\n11\t  recentRuns: Array<{ id: string; name: string; status: string; createdAt: string; durationMs: number }>;\n12\t  successRate: number;\n13\t  totalRuns24h: number;\n14\t  failedRuns: number;\n15\t}\n16\t\n17\texport interface WorkbenchAlertData {\n18\t  unread: number;\n19\t  critical: number;\n20\t  recent: Array<{ id: string; severity: string; message: string; createdAt: string; acknowledged: boolean }>;\n21\t}\n22\t\n23\texport interface WorkbenchTicketData {\n24\t  active: number;\n25\t  overdue: number;\n26\t  recent: Array<{ id: string; title: string; priority: string; status: string; slaRemaining: number }>;\n27\t}\n28\t\n29\texport interface WorkbenchDeploymentData {\n30\t  recent: Array<{ id: string; environment: string; status: string; version: string; deployedAt: string }>;\n31\t  successRate: number;\n32\t}\n33\t\n34\texport interface WorkbenchData {\n35\t  myPipelines: WorkbenchPipelineData;\n36\t  myAlerts: WorkbenchAlertData;\n37\t  myTickets: WorkbenchTicketData;\n38\t  myDeployments: WorkbenchDeploymentData;\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\texport class WorkbenchService {\n42\t  constructor(private pool: DatabasePool) {}\n43\t\n44\t  async getWorkbench(userId: string, tenantId: string = 'default'): Promise<WorkbenchData> {\n45\t    const [pipelines, alerts, tickets, deployments] = await Promise.allSettled([\n46\t      this.getPipelineData(userId, tenantId),\n47\t      this.getAlertData(userId, tenantId),\n48\t      this.getTicketData(userId, tenantId),\n49\t      this.getDeploymentData(userId, tenantId),\n50\t    ]);\n51\t\n52\t    return {\n53\t      myPipelines: pipelines.status === 'fulfilled' ? pipelines.value : {\n54\t        recentRuns: [], successRate: 0, totalRuns24h: 0, failedRuns: 0,\n55\t      },\n56\t      myAlerts: alerts.status === 'fulfilled' ? alerts.value : {\n57\t        unread: 0, critical: 0, recent: [],\n58\t      },\n59\t      myTickets: tickets.status === 'fulfilled' ? tickets.value : {\n60\t        active: 0, overdue: 0, recent: [],\n61\t      },\n62\t      myDeployments: deployments.status === 'fulfilled' ? deployments.value : {\n63\t        recent: [], successRate: 0,\n64\t      },\n65\t    };\n66\t  }\n67\t\n68\t  private async getPipelineData(_userId: string, _tenantId: string): Promise<WorkbenchPipelineData> {\n69\t    const runsResult = await this.pool.query(\n70\t      `SELECT id, name, status, created_at, duration_ms\n71\t       FROM pipeline_runs\n72\t       WHERE tenant_id = $1\n73\t       ORDER BY created_at DESC\n74\t       LIMIT 5`,\n75\t      [_tenantId]\n76\t    );\n77\t\n78\t    const statsResult = await this.pool.query(\n79\t      `SELECT\n80\t         COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE created_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '24 hours') AS total_24h,\n81\t         COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE created_at > string) => void;\n36\t  reject: (error: Error) => void;\n37\t};\n38\tlet failedQueue: PendingRequest[] = [];\n39\t\n40\tconst processQueue = (error: Error | null, token: string | null = null) => {\n41\t  failedQueue.forEach((promise) => {\n42\t    if (error) {\n43\t      promise.reject(error);\n44\t    } else if (token) {\n45\t      promise.resolve(token);\n46\t    }\n47\t  });\n48\t  failedQueue = [];\n49\t};\n50\t\n51\t// 响应拦截器 — 带自动 Token 刷新\n52\tapiClient.interceptors.response.use(\n53\t  (response: AxiosResponse<ApiResponse>) => {\n54\t    // 处理不一致的 API 响应格式\n55\t    // 部分接口返回 useAuthStore.getState().logout();\n73\t        // 不再做 window.location.href 跳转，让调用方的 catch 处理\n74\t        return Promise.reject(error);\n75\t      }\n76\t\n77\t      if (isRefreshing) {\n78\t originalRequest._retry = true;\n95\t      isRefreshing = true;\n96\t\n97\t      try {\n98\t        const refreshToken = localStorage.getItem('refresh_token');\n99\t        if (!refreshToken) {\n100\t          throw new Error('No refresh token available');\n101\t        }\n102\t\n103\t        // 直接调用刷新端点（绕过当前 axios 实例的拦截器，避免递归）\n104\t        const response = await axios.post(\n105\t {"parentUuid":"78ffb910-6039-414f-8809-d71f4040203a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c14fcd7d-c1c7-4cad-b300-9bc85d47d67f","agentId":"afb517f0e340c4912","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_366a68c6faa8409ab039cca9","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Alert Management API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * 告警关联、去重、抑制功能\n5\t *\n6\t * Prefix: /api/v1/alert\n7\t *\n8\t * Migrated to replyHelper (2026-05-21)\n9\t */\n10\t\n11\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n12\timport { AlertCorrelationService } from '../services/alert/AlertCorrelationService';\n13\timport { AlertDeduplication } from '../services/alert/AlertDeduplication';\n14\timport { AlertSuppressionService } from '../services/alert/AlertSuppressionService';\n15\timport { AlertStatus, Alert, AlertSeverity } from '../services/alert/AlertTypes';\n16\timport { success, created, badRequest, notFound, internalError } from '../utils/replyHelper';\n17\timport { ErrorCodes } from '../types/error-codes';\n18\t\n19\texport default async function alertRoutes(app: FastifyInstance): Promise<void> {\n20\t  // Initialize services\n21\t  const correlationService = new AlertCorrelationService();\n22\t  const deduplication = new AlertDeduplication();\n23\t  const suppressionService = new AlertSuppressionService();\n24\t\n25\t  // Start deduplication service\n26\t  deduplication.start();\n27\t\n28\t  // ==================== Alert Ingestion ====================\n29\t  // POST /alert/ingest - 接收告警\n30\t  app.post('/ingest', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n31\t    const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n32\t    try {\n33\t      const alert: Alert = {\n34\t        id: `alert-${Date.now()}`,\n35\t        fingerprint: '',\n36\t        name: body.name as string,\n37\t        severity: body.severity as AlertSeverity,\n38\t        status: AlertStatus.FIRING,\n39\t        sourceType: body.sourceType as any,\n40\t        sourceId: body.sourceId as string,\n41\t        sourceName: body.sourceName as string,\n42\t        labels: (body.labels as Record<string, string>) || {},\n43\t        annotations: (body.annotations as Record<string, string>) || {},\n44\t        value: (body.value as number) || 0,\n45\t        threshold: (body.threshold as number) || 0,\n46\t        tenantId: (body.tenantId as string) || 'default',\n47\t        startsAt: new Date(),\n48\t        createdAt: new Date(),\n49\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n50\t      };\n51\t\n52\t      // Generate fingerprint\n53\t      const fingerprint = deduplication.generateFingerprint(alert);\n54\t      alert.fingerprint = fingerprint.fingerprint;\n55\t\n56\t      // Check suppression\n57\t      const suppressionResult = await suppressionService.processAlert(alert);\n58\t      if (suppressionResult.suppressed) {\n59\t        return success(reply, request, {\n60\t          status: 'suppressed',\n61\t          reason: suppressionResult.reason,\n62\t          alert,\n63\t        });\n64\t      }\n65\t\n66\t      // Process deduplication\n67\t      const processResult = deduplication.processAlert(alert);\n68\t      return created(reply, request, {\n69\t        status: processResult.action === 'create' ? 'created' : 'updated',\n70\t        alert,\n71\t        isDuplicate: processResult.isDuplicate,\n72\t      });\n73\t    } catch (error) {\n74\t      return internalError(reply, request, error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Alert ingest failed');\n75\t    }\n76\t  });\n77\t\n78\t  // ==================== Alert Correlation ====================\n79\t  // POST /alert/correlate - 告警关联分析\n80\t  app.post('/correlate', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n81\t    const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n82\t    try {\n83\t      const alerts = body.alerts as Alert[];\n84\t      if (!alerts || alerts.length === 0) {\n85\t        return badRequest(reply, request, ErrorCodes.CLIENT_PARAM_INVALID, 'alerts is required');\n86\t      }\n87\t\n88\t      // Update topology health\n89\t      correlationService.updateNodeHealth(alerts);\n90\t\n91\t      // Perform correlation analysis\n92\t      const analysis = correlationService.analyzeRootCause(alerts);\n93\t      return success(reply, request, { analysis });\n94\t    } catch (error) {\n95\t      return internalError(reply, request, error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Correlation analysis failed');\n96\t    }\n97\t  });\n98\t\n99\t  // GET /alert/topology - 获取告警拓扑图\n100\t  app.get('/topology', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n101\t    const topology = correlationService.getTopology();\n102\t    return success(reply, request, { topology });\n103\t  });\n104\t\n105\t  // POST /alert/topology - 设置告警拓扑图\n106\t  app.post('/topology', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n107\t    const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n108\t    try {\n109\t      correlationService.setTopology(body as any);\n110\t      return success(reply, request, {\n111\t        status: 'updated',\n112\t        nodeCount: (body.nodes as any[])?.length || 0,\n113\t        edgeCount: (body.edges as any[])?.length || 0,\n114\t      });\n115\t    } catch (error) {\n116\t      return badRequest(reply, request, ErrorCodes.CLIENT_PARAM_INVALID, error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Invalid topology');\n117\t    }\n118\t  });\n119\t\n120\t  // ==================== Alert Deduplication ====================\n121\t  // GET /alert/deduplication/stats - 获取去重统计\n122\t  app.get('/deduplication/stats', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n123\t    const stats = deduplication.getStats();\n124\t    return success(reply, request, { stats });\n125\t  });\n126\t\n127\t  // GET /alert/groups - 获取告警分组\n128\t  app.get('/groups', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n129\t    const groups = deduplication.getActiveGroups();\n130\t    return success(reply, request, { groups });\n131\t  });\n132\t\n133\t  // ==================== Alert Suppression ====================\n134\t  // GET /alert/suppression/stats - 获取抑制统计\n135\t  app.get('/suppression/stats', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n136\t    const stats = suppressionService.getStats();\n137\t    return success(reply, request, { stats });\n138\t  });\n139\t\n140\t  // GET /alert/suppression/maintenance-windows - 获取维护窗口\n141\t  app.get('/suppression/maintenance-windows', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n142\t    const windows = suppressionService.getActiveMaintenanceWindows();\n143\t    return success(reply, request, { windows });\n144\t  });\n145\t\n146\t  // POST /alert/suppression/maintenance-windows - 添加维护窗口\n147\t  app.post('/suppression/maintenance-windows', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n148\t    const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n149\t    try {\n150\t      suppressionService.addMaintenanceWindow({\n151\t        name: body.name as string,\n152\t        description: body.description as string,\n153\t        startTime: new Date(body.startTime as string),\n154\t        endTime: new Date(body.endTime as string),\n155\t        tenantId: (body.tenantId as string) || 'default',\n156\t        scope: (body.scope as any) || {},\n157\t        createdBy: (body.createdBy as string) || 'system',\n158\t      });\n159\t      return created(reply, request, { status: 'created' });\n160\t    } catch (error) {\n161\t      return badRequest(reply, request, ErrorCodes.CLIENT_PARAM_INVALID, error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Invalid maintenance window');\n162\t    }\n163\t  });\n164\t\n165\t  // GET /alert/suppression/known-issues - 获取已知问题\n166\t  app.get('/suppression/known-issues', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n167\t    const issues = suppressionService.getOpenKnownIssues();\n168\t    return success(reply, request, { issues });\n169\t  });\n170\t\n171\t  // POST /alert/suppression/known-issues - 添加已知问题\n172\t  app.post('/suppression/known-issues', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n173\t    const body = request.body as Record<string, unknown>;\n174\t    try {\n175\t      suppressionService.addKnownIssue({\n176\t        title: body.title as string,\n177\t        description: body.description as string,\n178\t        tenantId: (body.tenantId as string) || 'default',\n179\t        fingerprintPattern: body.fingerprintPattern as string,\n180\t        labelSelectors: body.labelSelectors as Record<string, string>,\n181\t        silenceDuration: (body.silenceDuration as number) || 3600000, // 1 hour default\n182\t        status: 'open',\n183\t        createdBy: (body.createdBy as string) || 'system',\n184\t      });\n185\t      return created(reply, request, { status: 'created' });\n186\t    } catch (error) {\n187\t      return badRequest(reply, request, ErrorCodes.CLIENT_PARAM_INVALID, error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Invalid known issue');\n188\t    }\n189\t  });\n190\t\n191\t  // GET /alert/suppression/alerts - 获取活跃告警\n192\t  // P0-6 Fix: Return active alerts instead of stats\n193\t  app.get('/suppression/alerts', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n194\t    const groups = deduplication.getActiveGroups();\n195\t    const allAlerts = groups.flatMap(g => g.alerts);\n196\t    return success(reply, request, { alerts: allAlerts, total: allAlerts.length });\n197\t  });\n198\t\n199\t  // ==================== Alert List ====================\n200\t  // GET /alert/list - 获取告警列表\n201\t  app.get('/list', async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n202\t    const query = request.query as Record<string, unknown>;\n203\t\n204\t    // Get active groups from deduplication service\n205\t    const groups = deduplication.getActiveGroups();\n206\t    const allAlerts = groups.flatMap(g => g.alerts);\n207\t\n208\t    // Filter by severity\n209\t    let filtered = allAlerts;\n210\t    if (query.severity) {\n211\t      filtered = allAlerts.filter((a) => a.severity === query.severity);\n212\t    }\n213\t\n214\t    // Filter by status\n215\t    if (query.status) {\n216\t      filtered = allAlerts.filter((a) => a.status === query.status);\n217\t    }\n218\t\n219\t    // Apply limit\n220\t    const limit = (query.limit as number) || 100;\n221\t    filtered = filtered.slice(0, limit);\n222\t\n223\t    return success(reply, request, {\n224\t      alerts: filtered,\n225\t      total: filtered.length,\n226\t    });\n227\t  });\n228\t\n229\t  // GET /alert/:id - 获取告警详情\n230\t  app.get('/:id', async (request: FastifyRequest<{ Params: { id: string } }>, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n231\t    const { id } = request.params;\n232\t    const groups = deduplication.getActiveGroups();\n233\t    const allAlerts = groups.flatMap(g => g.alerts);\n234\t    const alert = allAlerts.find((a) => a.id === id);\n235\t\n236\t    if (!alert) {\n237\t      return notFound(reply, request, ErrorCodes.CLIENT_RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND, `Alert ${id} not found`);\n238\t    }\n239\t\n240\t    return success(reply, request, { alert });\n241\t  });\n242\t}\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.\n</system-reminder>\n"}]},"uuid":"ffe3a8b9-98da-4dd8-80ea-fd7b987bc431","timestamp":"2026-05-22T08:08:35.455Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"78ffb910-6039-414f-8809-d71f4040203a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/heal/orion-design","sessionId":"a156151f-9cba-4283-a721-280c456f81e0","version":"0.4.10","gitBranch":"feat/frontend-gap-implementation","slug":"wild-snuggling-thimble"}

* Smart Deploy API Routes\n3\t *\n4\t * Routes under /api/v1/deploy\n5\t * Handles deployment execution, history, metrics, and rollback\n6\t */\n7\t\n8\timport { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest, FastifyReply } from 'fastify';\n9\timport { DatabasePool } from '../services/database';\n10\timport { DeployController } from './controllers/DeployController';\n11\timport { SmartDeployService } from '../services/smart-deploy/SmartDeployService';\n12\timport { authenticateUser } from '../middleware/authMiddleware';\n13\timport { requirePermission } from '../middleware/requirePermission';\n14\t\n15\tinterface DeployRoutesOptions {\n16\t  database?: DatabasePool;\n17\t}\n18\t\n19\texport default async function deployRoutes(\n20\t  app: FastifyInstance,\n21\t  options: DeployRoutesOptions\n22\t): Promise<void> {\n23\t  if (!options.database) {\n24\t    console.warn('[DeployRoutes] No database pool provided, routes will not be functional');\n25\t    return;\n26\t  }\n27\t\n28\t  // Initialize service (SmartDeployService creates repositories internally)\n29\t  const smartDeployService = new Execution ====================\n35\t\n36\t  app.post('/deploy', {\n37\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'write' })]\n38\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n39\t    return controller.deploy(request, reply);\n40\t  });\n41\t\n42\t  // ==================== Deployment Status ====================\n43\t\n44\t  app.get('/deploy/:id', {\n45\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n46\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n47\t    return controller.getStatus(request, reply);\n48\t  });\n49\t\n50\t  // ==================== Deployment History ====================\n51\t\n52\t  app.get('/deploy/history', {\n53\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n54\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n55\t    return controller.getHistory(request, reply);\n56\t  });\n57\t\n58\t  app.get('/deploy/latest/:appName/:environment', {\n59\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })],\n60\t    schema: {\n61\t      params: {\n62\t        type: 'object',\n63\t        required: ['appName', 'environment'],\n64\t        properties: {\n65\t          appName: { type: 'string', minLength: 1, maxLength: 100 },\n66\t          environment: { type: 'string', enum: ['dev', 'staging', 'prod', 'development', 'production', 'pre-prod'] }\n67\t        }\n68\t      }\n69\t    }\n70\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n71\t    return controller.getLatestDeployment(request, reply);\n72\t  });\n73\t\n74\t  // ==================== Deployment Metrics ====================\n75\t\n76\t  app.get('/deploy/metrics', {\n77\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n78\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n79\t    return controller.getMetrics(request, reply);\n80\t  });\n81\t\n82\t  // ==================== Rollback ====================\n83\t\n84\t  app.post('/deploy/:id/rollback', {\n85\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'write' })]\n86\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n87\t    return controller.rollback(request, reply);\n88\t  });\n89\t\n90\t  app.get('/deploy/:id/rollbacks', {\n91\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n92\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n93\t    return controller.getRollbackHistory(request, reply);\n94\t  });\n95\t\n96\t  // ==================== Cancel ====================\n97\t\n98\t  app.post('/deploy/:id/cancel', {\n99\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'write' })]\n100\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n101\t    return controller.cancel(request, reply);\n102\t  });\n103\t\n104\t  // ==================== Audit Trail ====================\n105\t\n106\t  app.get('/deploy/:id/audit', {\n107\t    onRequest: [authenticateUser, requirePermission({ resource: 'deploy', action: 'read' })]\n108\t  }, async (request: FastifyRequest, reply: FastifyReply) => {\n109\t    return controller.getAuditTrail(request, reply);\n110\t  });\n111\t\n112\t  console.log('[DeployRoutes] Registered all deployment routes');\n113\t}\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read
